+$env:JAVA_HOME=$JavaHome

+if($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0){throw 'Compilation failed; see compile.log'}
+& python (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'wd007/run-checks.py') --java (Join-Path $JavaHome 'bin/java.exe') --output (Join-Path $OutputRoot 'behavior')

+if($behaviorExit -ne 0){throw 'One or more behavior suites failed; see behavior/RESULTS.json'}
+Write-Output "PASS WD-007 machine checks: $OutputRoot (not hardware/UAT or package admission)"

### scripts/wd007/ManagedBridge.cs
--- start/scripts/wd007/ManagedBridge.cs
+++ current/scripts/wd007/ManagedBridge.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,217 @@
+using System;
+using System.Collections.Generic;
+using System.IO;
+using System.Linq;
+using System.Net;
+using System.Net.Sockets;
+using System.Text;
+using System.Threading;
+using System.Threading.Tasks;
+using System.Web.Script.Serialization;
+using Windows.Devices.Bluetooth;
+using Windows.Devices.Bluetooth.GenericAttributeProfile;
+using Windows.Devices.Enumeration;
+
+namespace BLE_tcp_driver {
+    internal sealed class SelectionSnapshot<T> {
+        private Dictionary<string,T> candidates=new Dictionary<string,T>();
+        public string Id { get; private set; } = "";
+        public string Publish(Dictionary<string,T> found,bool observationOnly,bool sessionActive) {
+            if(!observationOnly && !sessionActive){candidates=new Dictionary<string,T>(found);Id=Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");}
+            return Id;
+        }
+        public bool Contains(string id,string target){return id==Id && candidates.ContainsKey(target);}
+    }
+    // Studio-only control plane. The legacy Form/TCP listener never starts in this mode.
+    internal sealed class ManagedBridge {
+        private readonly BleCore core = new BleCore();
+        private readonly object gate = new object(), outputGate = new object();
+        private readonly string instance = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
+        private readonly string token = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("AHAKEY_BRIDGE_TOKEN");
+        private readonly HashSet<string> requestIds = new HashSet<string>();
+        private readonly SelectionSnapshot<DeviceInformation> selection=new SelectionSnapshot<DeviceInformation>();
+        private readonly BoundedAsyncFifo<Write> writes;
+        private string session = "", target = "", clientGeneration = "";
+        private long epoch, eventSequence;
+        private TcpClient owner;
+        private CancellationTokenSource cancellation = new CancellationTokenSource();
+        private bool authenticated;
+        private int outstanding;
+        private sealed class Write {
+            public string Session;
+            public long Generation;
+            public GattCharacteristic Characteristic;
+            public byte[] Bytes;
+            public CancellationToken Token;
+        }
+        public ManagedBridge() {
+            writes = new BoundedAsyncFifo<Write>(24, SendWrite, (w,e) => {});
+            core.ReceiveNotifyData += (sender, data, generation, discovery) => {
+                lock (gate) {
+                    if (session.Length == 0 || !core.IsCurrentConnection(generation)) return;
+                    Event("notify", Map("bytes", Convert.ToBase64String(data)));
+                }
+            };
+            core.DeviceDisconnected += device => {
+                long captured=Interlocked.Read(ref epoch);
+                // BleCore invokes this while holding its discovery lock. Never acquire gate on that stack.
+                _ = Task.Run(() => { lock(gate) {if(captured!=epoch || !ReferenceEquals(device,core.CurrentDevice))return;Event("sessionDisconnected",Map());Revoke();} });
+            };
+        }
+        public static void Run(string[] args) {
+            int index = Array.IndexOf(args, "--port"); int port;
+            if (index < 0 || index + 1 >= args.Length || !int.TryParse(args[index + 1], out port) || port < 1 || port > 65535) return;
+            new ManagedBridge().RunAsync(port).GetAwaiter().GetResult();
+        }
+        private async Task RunAsync(int port) {
+            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(token)) return;
+            var listener = new TcpListener(IPAddress.Loopback, port);
+            try {
+                listener.Start(1);
+                var accept = listener.AcceptTcpClientAsync();
+                if (await Task.WhenAny(accept, Task.Delay(5000)) != accept) return;
+                owner = await accept; listener.Stop(); owner.NoDelay = true; owner.SendTimeout = 2000;
+                var stream = owner.GetStream();
+                while (true) {
+                    byte[] header = authenticated ? await Read(stream,3) : await Deadline(Read(stream,3),2000);
+                    int length = header[1] | header[2] << 8;
+                    if (header[0] != 0x10 || length < 2 || length > 16384) throw new IOException("BAD_FRAME");
+                    var data = new JavaScriptSerializer().DeserializeObject(Encoding.UTF8.GetString(await Deadline(Read(stream, length),2000))) as Dictionary<string, object>;
+                    if (data == null || Int(data, "version") != 1) throw new IOException("BAD_VERSION");
+                    string id = Text(data, "requestId"), op = Text(data, "op");
+                    if (!requestIds.Add(id) || requestIds.Count > 100000) { Reply(id, false, "DUPLICATE_REQUEST"); continue; }
+                    if (!authenticated) {
+                        if (op != "hello" || Text(data, "ownerToken") != token) throw new IOException("OWNER_MISMATCH");
+                        authenticated = true;
+                        Reply(id, true, "OK", Map("capabilities", new[]{"enumerate-connected","select-target","release-session","session-writes"}));
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    if (Text(data,"bridgeInstanceId") != instance) { Reply(id, false, "INSTANCE_CHANGED"); continue; }
+                    // Cancellation is handled here, never queued behind a GATT write.
+                    if (op == "disconnect" || op == "cancelRequest" || op == "shutdown") {
+                        lock (gate) {
+                            if (op == "disconnect" && session.Length != 0 && Text(data,"sessionId") != session) { Reply(id,false,"SESSION_CHANGED"); continue; }
+                            Revoke();
+                        }
+                        Reply(id,true,"REVOKED",Map("revoked",true));
+                        if (op == "shutdown") return;
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    if (Interlocked.Increment(ref outstanding) > 24) { Interlocked.Decrement(ref outstanding); Reply(id,false,"BUSY"); continue; }
+                    _ = Task.Run(async () => {
+                        try { await Handle(id, op, data); }
+                        catch (Exception e) { Reply(id,false,e is TimeoutException ? "TIMEOUT" : e.Message); }
+                        finally { Interlocked.Decrement(ref outstanding); }
+                    });
+                }
+            } catch (Exception) { }
+            finally { listener.Stop(); lock(gate) Revoke(); owner?.Close(); }
+        }
+        private async Task Handle(string id, string op, Dictionary<string,object> data) {
+            if (op == "enumerateConnected") {
+                bool observationOnly=data.ContainsKey("observationOnly") && data["observationOnly"] is bool && (bool)data["observationOnly"];
+                long captured; lock(gate) { captured=epoch; }
+                var found = await Deadline(DeviceInformation.FindAllAsync(BluetoothLEDevice.GetDeviceSelectorFromConnectionStatus(BluetoothConnectionStatus.Connected),
+                    new[]{"System.Devices.Aep.DeviceAddress","System.Devices.Aep.IsConnected"}).AsTask(), 4500);
+                var list = new List<Dictionary<string,object>>();
+                var discovered=new Dictionary<string,DeviceInformation>();
+                lock (gate) {
+                    if (captured!=epoch) throw new IOException("CANCELLED");
+                    foreach (var d in found) {
+                        object connected;
+                        if (!d.Name.StartsWith("AhaKey",StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
+                            || !d.Properties.TryGetValue("System.Devices.Aep.IsConnected",out connected) || !(connected is bool) || !(bool)connected) continue;
+                        discovered[d.Id]=d;
+                        list.Add(Map("targetId",d.Id,"name",d.Name,"observedConnected",true,"serviceEvidence","connect-verifies-services"));
+                    }
+                    Reply(id,true,"OK",Map("snapshotId",selection.Publish(discovered,observationOnly,session.Length!=0),"candidates",list));
+                }
+                return;
+            }
+            if (op == "connect") {
+                string wanted=Text(data,"targetId"), generation=Text(data,"clientGeneration"); long captured;
+                lock(gate) {
+                    if(session.Length!=0 || !selection.Contains(Text(data,"snapshotId"),wanted)) throw new IOException("TARGET_CHANGED");
+                    captured=Interlocked.Increment(ref epoch); session="connecting"; target=wanted; clientGeneration=generation;
+                }
+                BluetoothLEDevice device=null;
+                try {
+                    var opening=BluetoothLEDevice.FromIdAsync(wanted).AsTask();
+                    try { device=await Deadline(opening,4000); }
+                    catch { _ = opening.ContinueWith(t=>{if(t.Status==TaskStatus.RanToCompletion)t.Result?.Dispose();}); throw; }
+                    if(device==null || device.ConnectionStatus!=BluetoothConnectionStatus.Connected || !device.Name.StartsWith("AhaKey",StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) throw new IOException("TARGET_NOT_CONNECTED");
+                    var ready=new TaskCompletionSource<bool>(TaskCreationOptions.RunContinuationsAsynchronously);
+                    Action<long,long,bool> callback=(g,d,ok)=>{
+                        if(ok) core.TryConfirmReady(g,d,(dev,ch)=>ready.TrySetResult(true));
+                        else ready.TrySetResult(false);
+                    };
+                    core.AllCharacteristicsDiscovered+=callback;
+                    try {
+                        lock(gate) { if(captured!=epoch)throw new IOException("CANCELLED"); core.ConnectManagedDevice(device); device=null; }
+                        if(!await Deadline(ready.Task,14000)) throw new IOException("SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE");
+                        lock(gate) {
+                            if(captured!=epoch || !core.GetBleStatusInfo().IsTargetDevice) throw new IOException("SESSION_CHANGED");
+                            session=Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
+                            Reply(id,true,"READY",Map("sessionId",session,"targetId",target,"clientGeneration",generation));
+                        }
+                    } finally { core.AllCharacteristicsDiscovered-=callback; }
+                } catch { lock(gate) {if(captured==epoch)Revoke();} throw; }
+                finally { device?.Dispose(); }
+                return;
+            }
+            if(op=="write") {
+                Write write;
+                lock(gate) {
+                    if(session.Length==0 || session=="connecting" || Text(data,"sessionId")!=session || Text(data,"targetId")!=target || Text(data,"clientGeneration")!=clientGeneration) throw new IOException("SESSION_CHANGED");
+                    byte[] bytes=Convert.FromBase64String(Text(data,"bytes"));
+                    string channel=Text(data,"channel");
+                    if(bytes.Length==0 || bytes.Length>4096 || (channel!="command" && channel!="data")) throw new IOException("BAD_WRITE");
+                    write=new Write{Session=session,Generation=core.ConnectionGeneration,Bytes=bytes,
+                        Characteristic=channel=="command"?core.CurrentWriteCharacteristic:core.CurrentDataCharacteristic,Token=cancellation.Token};
+                }
+                Task<bool> completion;
+                if(!writes.TryEnqueue(write,out completion))throw new IOException("BUSY");
+                if(!await Deadline(completion,10000))throw new IOException("WRITE_UNCONFIRMED");
+                Reply(id,true,"TRANSPORT_WRITTEN",Map("transportWritten",true)); return;
+            }
+            throw new IOException("UNKNOWN_OPERATION");
+        }
+        private async Task<bool> SendWrite(Write w) {
+            using(var timeout=CancellationTokenSource.CreateLinkedTokenSource(w.Token)) {
+                timeout.CancelAfter(5000);
+                int pdu; string source; int size=core.GetMaxGattWritePayload(w.Generation,out pdu,out source);
+                return await BridgeMtuPolicy.SendFragmentsAsync(w.Bytes,size,async(offset,bytes,index,count)=>{
+                    if(!Current(w)||timeout.IsCancellationRequested)return false;
+                    var result=await core.WriteDataToCharacteristicAsync(w.Characteristic,bytes,w.Generation,timeout.Token);
+                    return result.Success && Current(w) && !timeout.IsCancellationRequested;
+                },()=>Current(w)&&!timeout.IsCancellationRequested);
+            }
+        }
+        private bool Current(Write w) { lock(gate)return session==w.Session && !w.Token.IsCancellationRequested && core.IsCurrentConnection(w.Generation); }
+        private void Revoke() {
+            Interlocked.Increment(ref epoch); session=""; target=""; clientGeneration="";
+            cancellation.Cancel(); cancellation=new CancellationTokenSource();
+            writes.CancelWhere(w=>true); core.Dispose();
+        }
+        private void Event(string name,Dictionary<string,object> values) {
+            values["event"]=name;values["eventSeq"]=++eventSequence;values["sessionId"]=session;
+            values["targetId"]=target;values["clientGeneration"]=clientGeneration; Send(0x91,values);
+        }
+        private void Reply(string id,bool ok,string code,Dictionary<string,object> values=null) {
+            values=values??Map();values["requestId"]=id;values["ok"]=ok;values["code"]=code;Send(0x90,values);
+        }
+        private void Send(byte type,Dictionary<string,object> values) {
+            try { lock(outputGate) {
+                values["version"]=1;values["bridgeInstanceId"]=instance;
+                byte[] body=Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(new JavaScriptSerializer().Serialize(values));
+                if(body.Length>16384)throw new IOException("FRAME_TOO_LARGE");
+                var stream=owner.GetStream();byte[] packet=new byte[body.Length+3];packet[0]=type;packet[1]=(byte)body.Length;packet[2]=(byte)(body.Length>>8);Buffer.BlockCopy(body,0,packet,3,body.Length);stream.Write(packet,0,packet.Length);
+            }} catch { owner?.Close(); }
+        }
+        private static async Task<byte[]> Read(NetworkStream stream,int count) {byte[] bytes=new byte[count];int at=0;while(at<count){int read=await stream.ReadAsync(bytes,at,count-at);if(read==0)throw new EndOfStreamException();at+=read;}return bytes;}
+        private static async Task<T> Deadline<T>(Task<T> task,int ms) {if(await Task.WhenAny(task,Task.Delay(ms))!=task)throw new TimeoutException();return await task;}
+        private static string Text(Dictionary<string,object> value,string key) {object item;if(!value.TryGetValue(key,out item)||!(item is string)||((string)item).Length>12000)throw new IOException("BAD_FIELD_"+key);return (string)item;}
+        private static int Int(Dictionary<string,object> value,string key) {object item;return value.TryGetValue(key,out item)&&item is int?(int)item:-1;}
+        private static Dictionary<string,object> Map(params object[] pairs) {var value=new Dictionary<string,object>();for(int i=0;i<pairs.Length;i+=2)value[(string)pairs[i]]=pairs[i+1];return value;}
+    }
+}

+# AhaKey Claude Hook - Auto-generated, do not edit
+param([Parameter(Position=0)][string]$EventName)
+. (Join-Path $env:USERPROFILE '.ahakey\hooks\ahakey-core.ps1')
+# Claude PermissionRequest: output hookSpecificOutput in Claude format
+if ($EventName -eq 'PermissionRequest') {
+    $isAuto = $response -match '"autoApproved"\s*:\s*true'
+    if ($isAuto) {
+        [Console]::WriteLine('{"hookSpecificOutput":{"hookEventName":"PermissionRequest","decision":{"behavior":"allow"}}}')
+    } else {
+        [Console]::WriteLine('{"hookSpecificOutput":{"hookEventName":"PermissionRequest","decision":{"behavior":"ask"}}}')
+    }
+    exit 0
+}
+# Claude lifecycle events: pass through server response
+if ($response) { [Console]::WriteLine($response) } else { [Console]::WriteLine('{"ok":true}') }
+exit 0

+import argparse, concurrent.futures, hashlib, json, subprocess, time
+from pathlib import Path
+
+parser=argparse.ArgumentParser()
+parser.add_argument('--java',required=True)
+parser.add_argument('--output',required=True)
+parser.add_argument('--filter',default='')
+args=parser.parse_args()
+output=Path(args.output).resolve();output.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False)
+classes=[
+ 'service.connection.ManagedBridgeClientChecks','service.Wd007BehaviorChecks','service.Wd007SafetyChecks','service.Wd007RecoveryControllerChecks','service.Wd007UsbTerminationChecks','service.Wd007BridgeRetirementChecks','service.Wd007WindowExitChecks','service.KimiBridgeLifecycleCheck',

### scripts/wd007/test-kimi-consumer.py
--- start/scripts/wd007/test-kimi-consumer.py
+++ current/scripts/wd007/test-kimi-consumer.py
@@ -0,0 +1,29 @@
+"""Replay the installed consumer's exact loader against Java's compatibility endpoint."""
+import argparse, hashlib, socket, struct, subprocess
+from pathlib import Path
+
+parser=argparse.ArgumentParser()
+parser.add_argument('--java',required=True)
+parser.add_argument('--consumer',required=True)
+args=parser.parse_args()
+source=Path(args.consumer).read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+loader=source[source.index('def _load_ahakey_override_uncached('):source.index('\ndef get_ahakey_approval_override(')]
+for state in ['auto','swapped-auto','custom','unknown','disconnected']:
+    child=subprocess.Popen([args.java,'-cp','target/test-classes;target/classes;target/lib/*','com.example.ahakey.service.KimiBridgeLifecycleCheck','consumer-probe',state],stdin=subprocess.PIPE,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,text=True,encoding='utf-8',errors='replace')
+    try:
+        port=None
+        for line in child.stdout:
+            if line.startswith('KIMI_PROBE_PORT='):
+                port=int(line.split('=')[1]);break
+        assert port is not None,'Java probe failed'
+        scope=dict(socket=socket,struct=struct,_AHAKEY_BRIDGE_HOST='127.0.0.1',_AHAKEY_BRIDGE_PORT=port)
+        exec(loader,scope)
+        result=scope['_load_ahakey_override_uncached']()
+        if state in ['auto','swapped-auto']:assert result and result['is_auto'] is True,(state,result)
+        else:assert result is None,(state,result)
+        print('PASS actual Kimi consumer:',state,repr(result))
+        child.communicate('\n',timeout=3)
+        assert child.returncode==0
+    finally:
+        if child.poll() is None:child.kill();child.wait(timeout=2)
+print('Consumer source SHA256:',hashlib.sha256(source.encode()).hexdigest())

### scripts/wd007/test-managed-ipc.py
--- start/scripts/wd007/test-managed-ipc.py
+++ current/scripts/wd007/test-managed-ipc.py
@@ -0,0 +1,63 @@
+"""Exercise only control-plane framing in our own compiled child; never enumerate/connect devices."""
+import argparse, hashlib, json, os, socket, struct, subprocess, time, uuid
+from pathlib import Path
+
+parser=argparse.ArgumentParser()
+parser.add_argument('--bridge',required=True)
+args=parser.parse_args()
+exe=Path(args.bridge).resolve()
+checks=0
+def check(value,name):
+    global checks
+    assert value,name
+    checks+=1
+def packet(peer,body):
+    raw=json.dumps(body).encode();peer.sendall(struct.pack('<BH',0x10,len(raw))+raw)
+def receive(peer):
+    def exact(n):
+        data=b''
+        while len(data)<n:
+            chunk=peer.recv(n-len(data))
+            if not chunk:raise EOFError()
+            data+=chunk
+        return data
+    kind,length=struct.unpack('<BH',exact(3));check(kind==0x90,'response envelope')
+    return json.loads(exact(length))
+def launch(token,port):
+    env=os.environ.copy();env['AHAKEY_BRIDGE_TOKEN']=token
+    return subprocess.Popen([str(exe),'--studio','--minimized','--port',str(port)],env=env,cwd=exe.parent,
+                            creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,stderr=subprocess.PIPE)
+for scenario in ['normal','wrong-token','owner-closes','invalid-frame']:
+    token=str(uuid.uuid4())
+    with socket.socket() as reservation:
+        reservation.bind(('127.0.0.1',0));port=reservation.getsockname()[1]
+    child=launch(token,port)
+    try:
+        peer=None;end=time.monotonic()+4
+        while peer is None and time.monotonic()<end:
+            try:peer=socket.create_connection(('127.0.0.1',port),timeout=.15)
+            except OSError:time.sleep(.025)
+        check(peer is not None,'managed listener starts without Form or device')
+        with peer:
+            peer.settimeout(3)
+            packet(peer,dict(version=1,requestId='1',op='hello',ownerToken=token if scenario!='wrong-token' else 'wrong',versions=[1]))
+            if scenario=='wrong-token':
+                check(peer.recv(1)==b'','unauthorized owner rejected')
+            else:
+                reply=receive(peer);check(reply['ok'] and 'session-writes' in reply['capabilities'],'versioned hello')
+                instance=reply['bridgeInstanceId']
+                if scenario=='normal':
+                    packet(peer,dict(version=1,requestId='2',op='write',bridgeInstanceId=instance,sessionId='absent',targetId='fake',clientGeneration='1',channel='command',bytes='qrsAzN0='))
+                    check(receive(peer)['code']=='SESSION_CHANGED','write without selected target rejected before GATT')
+                    packet(peer,dict(version=1,requestId='2',op='write',bridgeInstanceId=instance))
+                    check(receive(peer)['code']=='DUPLICATE_REQUEST','duplicate never replays')
+                    packet(peer,dict(version=1,requestId='3',op='write',bridgeInstanceId='old'))
+                    check(receive(peer)['code']=='INSTANCE_CHANGED','old instance fenced')
+                    packet(peer,dict(version=1,requestId='4',op='shutdown',bridgeInstanceId=instance))
+                    check(receive(peer)['revoked'],'shutdown revokes even without a device')
+                elif scenario=='invalid-frame':
+                    peer.sendall(bytes([0x10,0xff,0xff]));check(peer.recv(1)==b'','oversized frame rejected')
+        check(child.wait(timeout=3)==0,'owned process terminates after owner closes or shutdown')
+    finally:
+        if child.poll() is None:child.kill();child.wait(timeout=2)
+print(json.dumps(dict(status='PASS',checks=checks,bridge=str(exe),sha256=hashlib.sha256(exe.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),scope='private IPC only; no enumeration or GATT'),ensure_ascii=False))

### scripts/wd007/test-managed-selection.ps1

+param([string]$Source=(Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'ManagedBridge.cs'))
+$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
+$text=[IO.File]::ReadAllText($Source)
+$start=$text.IndexOf('internal sealed class SelectionSnapshot<T>')
+if($start -lt 0){throw 'Missing actual selection class'}
+$brace=$text.IndexOf('{',$start);$depth=0;$end=-1
+for($i=$brace;$i -lt $text.Length;$i++){
+    if($text[$i] -eq '{'){$depth++}
+    if($text[$i] -eq '}'){$depth--;if($depth -eq 0){$end=$i;break}}
+}
+if($end -lt 0){throw 'Unbalanced actual class'}
+$actual=$text.Substring($start,$end-$start+1)
+Add-Type -TypeDefinition (@'
+using System;
+using System.Collections.Generic;
+'@ + $actual + @'
+public static class SelectionProbe {
+    public static void Run() {
+        var selection=new SelectionSnapshot<string>();
+        var sameNames=new Dictionary<string,string>{{"id-A","AhaKey"},{"id-B","AhaKey"}};
+        string foreground=selection.Publish(sameNames,false,false);
+        selection.Publish(new Dictionary<string,string>{{"id-C","AhaKey"}},true,false);
+        if(!selection.Contains(foreground,"id-A")||!selection.Contains(foreground,"id-B")||selection.Contains(foreground,"id-C"))throw new Exception("late background overwrote user choice");
+        selection.Publish(new Dictionary<string,string>(),true,false);
+        if(!selection.Contains(foreground,"id-B"))throw new Exception("empty observation invalidated selection");
+        selection.Publish(new Dictionary<string,string>(),false,true);
+        if(!selection.Contains(foreground,"id-A"))throw new Exception("active session changed selection");
+        string fresh=selection.Publish(new Dictionary<string,string>{{"id-C","AhaKey"}},false,false);
+        if(selection.Contains(foreground,"id-A")||selection.Contains(fresh,"id-A")||!selection.Contains(fresh,"id-C"))throw new Exception("fresh foreground did not replace old candidates");
+    }
+}
+'@)
+[SelectionProbe]::Run()
+Write-Output ('PASS actual C# selection class: foreground/background reverse completion, duplicate names, empty observation, active session, stale choice. SHA256='+(Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $Source).Hash)

     public static String getName(int code) {
+        if((code & 0xFF00)!=0) {
+            var parts=new java.util.ArrayList<String>();
+            int[] bits={0x200,0x100,0x400,0x800,0x2000,0x1000,0x4000,0x8000};
+            String[] names={"Ctrl","Shift","Alt","Win","RCtrl","RShift","RAlt","RWin"};
+            for(int i=0;i<bits.length;i++)if((code&bits[i])!=0)parts.add(names[i]);
+            if((code&255)!=0)parts.add(getName(code&255));return String.join("+",parts);
+        }
         return codeToName.getOrDefault(code, String.format("0x%02X", code));
     }

+/** FX-thread state; dirty ownership is (mode,K1), never dirtyModes x dirtyParts. */
+public final class K1RouteState {
+    public enum Capability { UNKNOWN, LEGACY, ROUTE }
+    public final ObjectProperty<Capability> capability = new SimpleObjectProperty<>(Capability.UNKNOWN);
+    public final IntegerProperty revision = new SimpleIntegerProperty();
+    public final BooleanProperty busy = new SimpleBooleanProperty();
+    public final StringProperty message = new SimpleStringProperty("K1能力尚未确认");
+    private final K1Route[] routes = new K1Route[4];
+    private final long[] edits = new long[4];
+    private final boolean[] dirty = new boolean[4];
+    private final boolean[] confirmed = new boolean[4];
+    private final String[] descriptions = new String[4];
+    private final boolean[] descriptionEdited = new boolean[4];
+    private long generation = -1;
+    public long generation() { return generation; }
+    public boolean confirmed(int mode) { return confirmed[mode]; }
+    public K1Route route(int mode) { return routes[mode]; }
+    public boolean dirty(int mode) { return dirty[mode]; }

+    public String description(int mode){return descriptions[mode];}

+    public void markDescriptionEdited(int mode){descriptionEdited[mode]=true;}
+    public void observedDescription(int mode,String description){descriptions[mode]=description;revision.set(revision.get()+1);}
+    public void markEdited(int mode) { dirty[mode]=true;edits[mode]++;revision.set(revision.get()+1); }
+    public void reset(long next) { generation=next;capability.set(Capability.UNKNOWN);java.util.Arrays.fill(confirmed,false);java.util.Arrays.fill(routes,null);java.util.Arrays.fill(descriptions,null);message.set("K1能力尚未确认");revision.set(revision.get()+1); }
+    record Draft(boolean[] dirty,long[] edits,boolean[] descriptionEdited) {}
+    Draft draft(){return new Draft(dirty.clone(),edits.clone(),descriptionEdited.clone());}
+    void restore(Draft draft){
+        for(int i=0;i<4;i++){dirty[i]=draft!=null && draft.dirty()[i];edits[i]=draft==null?0:draft.edits()[i];descriptionEdited[i]=draft!=null&&draft.descriptionEdited()[i];}
+        busy.set(false);reset(-1);
+    }
+    public void observed(int mode,K1Route route) { routes[mode]=route;confirmed[mode]=true;revision.set(revision.get()+1); }
+    public void failed(int mode,String reason) { confirmed[mode]=false;message.set(reason);revision.set(revision.get()+1); }
+    public void saved(int mode,long edit,K1Route route) { observed(mode,route);if(edits[mode]==edit){dirty[mode]=false;descriptionEdited[mode]=false;}revision.set(revision.get()+1); }
+    public void savedLegacy(int mode,long edit) { if(edits[mode]==edit){dirty[mode]=false;descriptionEdited[mode]=false;}revision.set(revision.get()+1); }

+    public void selectDevice(String identity) {
+        if(identity==null || identity.isBlank() || identity.equals(draftTarget))return;

+        for(var item:keyConfigs.entrySet()) {
+            var keys=new EnumMap<StudioPart,KeyConfig>(StudioPart.class);
+            item.getValue().forEach((part,key)->keys.put(part,key.copy()));saved.put(item.getKey(),keys);
+        }
+        deviceDrafts.put(draftTarget,new DeviceDraft(saved,sideSwitchConfig,dirtyParts.clone(),dirtyModes.clone(),k1Routes.draft()));
+        DeviceDraft draft=deviceDrafts.get(identity);draftTarget=identity;
+        dirtyParts.clear();dirtyModes.clear();
+        for(ModeSlot mode:ModeSlot.values()) {
+            var keys=keyConfigs.get(mode);keys.clear();
+            for(StudioPart part:new StudioPart[]{StudioPart.KEY1,StudioPart.KEY2,StudioPart.KEY3,StudioPart.KEY4})
+                keys.put(part,draft==null?new KeyConfig(0,"未读回"):draft.keys().get(mode).get(part).copy());
+        }
+        sideSwitchConfig=draft==null?defaultSideSwitchConfig():draft.side();
+        if(draft!=null){dirtyParts.addAll(draft.parts());dirtyModes.addAll(draft.modes());}
+        k1Routes.restore(draft==null?null:draft.k1());
+        dirtyCount.set(dirtyParts.size());inputReadModes.clear();syncing.set(false);modeSwitching.set(false);
+        inputReadbackInProgress.set(false);inputFailureStatus.set("");inputReadbackStatus.set("正在读取当前键盘配置...");

+package com.example.ahakey.model.connection;
+
+public record DeviceTarget(String transport, String id, String name) {
+    public DeviceTarget {
+        if (!(transport.equals("USB") || transport.equals("BLE")) || id == null || id.isBlank())
+            throw new IllegalArgumentException("设备身份无效");
+    }
+    public String key() { return transport + ":" + id; }
+    @Override public String toString() { return name + " · " + transport + " · " + id; }
+}

+    public synchronized void simulateAction(com.example.ahakey.model.KeyConfig key) {
+        if (simulationThread != null && simulationThread.isAlive()) {
+            lastSimulateHint.set("正在模拟，请等待本次动作结束。"); return;
+        }

+        catch (IllegalArgumentException e) { lastSimulateHint.set(e.getMessage()); return; }

+            String result;
+            try {
+                com.example.ahakey.service.KeySimulationService.execute(action, (hid, down) -> {
+                    int vk = hidBaseToVk(hid);
+                    if (vk < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("不支持的模拟键: " + hid);
+                    WinUser.INPUT input = new WinUser.INPUT();
+                    fillKey(input, vk, !down);
+                    int count = User32.INSTANCE.SendInput(new WinUser.DWORD(1), new WinUser.INPUT[]{input}, input.size()).intValue();
+                    if (count != 1) throw new IllegalStateException("系统未接受按键输入");
+                }, Thread::sleep);
+                result = "模拟输入已发送；目标应用是否执行请实际确认。";
+            } catch (Exception e) { result = "模拟未完成：" + e.getMessage(); }
+            String message = result;
+            Platform.runLater(() -> lastSimulateHint.set(message));
+        }, "key-simulation");
+        simulationThread.setDaemon(true); simulationThread.start();
     }
 
     private static int hidBaseToVk(int hid) {
+        if (hid >= 0xE0 && hid <= 0xE7) return new int[]{0xA2,0xA0,0xA4,0x5B,0xA3,0xA1,0xA5,0x5C}[hid - 0xE0];
         // 字母 A(0x04)–Z(0x1D) → VK 0x41–0x5A
         if (hid >= 0x04 && hid <= 0x1D) return 0x41 + (hid - 0x04);
         // 数字 1(0x1E)–9(0x26) → VK 0x31–0x39; 0(0x27) → 0x30

+    private void openSelected(com.example.ahakey.model.connection.DeviceTarget target,long intent) throws Exception {
+        long generation;
+        java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture<Void> release;
+        Object releaseOwner;
+        UsbHidTransport openedUsb = createUsbTransport();
+        String openedSession = "";
+        commandLock.lock();
+        try {
+            // The intent check and revocation are one transition: an obsolete queued attempt has no rights.
+            if(intent!=connectionIntent.get() || !maintenance.normalAllowed())return;
+            disconnectInternal();
+            activeTarget=target; displayWriteUncertain=recoveryTargets.contains(target.key());
+            generation=connectionGeneration; release=bridgeRelease;releaseOwner=bridgeReleaseOwner;
+            if(target.transport().equals("USB")){
+                usbTransport=openedUsb;
+                openedUsb.onUnexpectedDisconnect(()->usbSessionTerminated(openedUsb,generation));
+            }
+        } finally { commandLock.unlock(); }
+        boolean published=false;
+        try {
+            try{release.get(2500,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);}
+            catch(java.util.concurrent.ExecutionException failure){
+                // Recheck the captured owner on each explicit retry; a historical failed Future is not current liveness.
+                if(!managedBridge.ownerRetired(releaseOwner))throw failure;
+                commandLock.lock();try{if(bridgeRelease==release){bridgeRelease=java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture.completedFuture(null);bridgeReleaseOwner=null;}}finally{commandLock.unlock();}
+            }
+            if(intent!=connectionIntent.get())return;
+            if(target.transport().equals("USB")) {
+                if(!usbCandidates().contains(target.id()))throw new IOException("USB目标已移除。");
+                var opening=transportIo.submit(()->{openedUsb.openExact(target.id(),bytes->onSessionNotify(generation,bytes));return null;});
+                try { opening.get(2500,TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS); }
+                catch(Exception failure) { opening.cancel(true); throw failure; }
+            } else {
+                var fields=com.example.ahakey.service.connection.ManagedBridgeClient.fields().put("targetId",target.id()).put("snapshotId",bridgeSnapshot).put("clientGeneration",Long.toString(generation));
+                var reply=managedBridge.request("connect",fields,15000);
+                openedSession=reply.path("sessionId").asText();
+                if(openedSession.isBlank())throw new IOException("蓝牙配置会话未就绪。");
+            }
+            commandLock.lock();
+            try {
+                if(intent!=connectionIntent.get() || generation!=connectionGeneration || !maintenance.normalAllowed())return;
+                if(target.transport().equals("USB")&&!openedUsb.isOpen())throw new IOException("USB读取会话已结束。");
+                usbTransport=openedUsb;bridgeSession=openedSession;
+                isConnected=true;isScanning=false;cachedStatus.setConnected(true);cachedStatus.setDeviceName(target.name());cachedStatus.clearFirmwareIdentity();

+    private void recoverAfterPhysicalReconnect(String target,long generation) throws Exception {

+    private void copyJsonStringField(String source, ObjectNode target, String fieldName) {
+        Matcher matcher = Pattern.compile("\\\\?\"" + Pattern.quote(fieldName) + "\\\\?\"\\s*:\\s*\\\\?\"([^\"\\\\]*(?:\\\\.[^\"\\\\]*)*)")
+            .matcher(source);
+        if (matcher.find()) {
+            target.put(fieldName, unescapeLooseJsonString(matcher.group(1)));
+        }
+    }
+
+    private String unescapeLooseJsonString(String value) {
+        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
+            return "";
+        }
+        return value
+            .replace("\\\\", "\\")
+            .replace("\\/", "/")
+            .replace("\\\"", "\"");
+    }

+    public void migrateOwnedApprovalScripts() {

+        if(!Files.isDirectory(hooks))return;

+                if(!Files.isRegularFile(file) || Files.isSymbolicLink(file))continue;
+                byte[] before=Files.readAllBytes(file);
+                String hash=java.util.HexFormat.of().formatHex(java.security.MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(before));

### src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/service/KeySimulationService.java
--- start/src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/service/KeySimulationService.java
+++ current/src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/service/KeySimulationService.java
@@ -0,0 +1,73 @@
+package com.example.ahakey.service;
+
+import com.example.ahakey.model.KeyConfig;
+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
+import java.util.List;
+
+/** Executes an immutable editor snapshot. This class never accesses a device or changes focus. */
+public final class KeySimulationService {
+    public record Step(String action, int value) {}
+    public record Action(List<Step> steps) {
+        public Action { steps = List.copyOf(steps); }
+    }
+    @FunctionalInterface public interface Sender { void send(int hid, boolean down) throws Exception; }
+    @FunctionalInterface public interface Delay { void waitMillis(long millis) throws InterruptedException; }
+
+    public static Action snapshot(KeyConfig key) {
+        List<Step> steps = new ArrayList<>();
+        if (key.usesMacro()) {
+            for (int i = 0; i < key.getMacroStepCount(); i++)
+                steps.add(new Step(key.getMacroStepAction(i), key.getMacroStepParam(i)));
+        } else {
+            int code = key.getHidCode();
+            int[] bits = {0x200, 0x100, 0x400, 0x800, 0x2000, 0x1000, 0x4000, 0x8000};
+            List<Integer> keys = new ArrayList<>();
+            for (int i = 0; i < bits.length; i++) if ((code & bits[i]) != 0) keys.add(0xE0 + i);
+            if ((code & 255) != 0) keys.add(code & 255);
+            for (int hid : keys) steps.add(new Step("DOWN_KEY", hid));
+            for (int i = keys.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--) steps.add(new Step("UP_KEY", keys.get(i)));
+        }
+        if (steps.isEmpty()) throw new IllegalArgumentException("未设置动作，无法模拟。");
+        for (Step step : steps) {
+            if (step.value < 0 || step.value > 255 || !List.of("DOWN_KEY", "UP_KEY", "DELAY").contains(step.action))
+                throw new IllegalArgumentException("宏步骤无效。");
+        }
+        return new Action(steps);
+    }
+
+    public static void execute(Action action, Sender sender, Delay delay) throws Exception {
+        LinkedHashSet<Integer> pressed = new LinkedHashSet<>();
+        Exception failure = null;
+        try {
+            for (Step step : action.steps) {
+                if (Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()) throw new InterruptedException("模拟已取消");
+                switch (step.action) {
+                    case "DELAY" -> delay.waitMillis(step.value * 3L);
+                    case "DOWN_KEY" -> {
+                        // A native failure may still have delivered the key; always attempt its release.
+                        pressed.add(step.value);
+                        sender.send(step.value, true);
+                    }
+                    case "UP_KEY" -> {
+                        // Do not release a key held by the user, outside this simulation.
+                        if (pressed.contains(step.value)) {
+                            sender.send(step.value, false);
+                            pressed.remove(step.value);
+                        }
+                    }
+                    default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("未知宏步骤");
+                }
+            }
+        } catch (Exception e) { failure = e; }
+        finally {
+            var keys = new ArrayList<>(pressed);
+            for (int i = keys.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
+                try { sender.send(keys.get(i), false); }
+                catch (Exception e) { if (failure == null) failure = e; else failure.addSuppressed(e); }
+            }
+        }
+        if (failure != null) throw failure;
+    }
+    private KeySimulationService() {}
+}

### src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/service/connection/DeviceRecoveryRegistry.java
--- start/src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/service/connection/DeviceRecoveryRegistry.java

+package com.example.ahakey.service.connection;
+
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.type.TypeReference;
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
+import java.io.IOException;
+import java.nio.file.*;
+import java.util.*;
+
+/** Durable uncertainty, keyed by exact transport identity. Restarting Studio is not device recovery. */
+public final class DeviceRecoveryRegistry {
+    private static final ObjectMapper JSON=new ObjectMapper();
+    private final Path file;
+    private final Map<String,Boolean> targets=new HashMap<>();
+    private boolean unreadable;
+    public DeviceRecoveryRegistry(Path file){
+        this.file=file;
+        try{if(Files.exists(file))targets.putAll(JSON.readValue(Files.readAllBytes(file),new TypeReference<Map<String,Boolean>>(){}));}
+        catch(Exception failure){unreadable=true;}
+    }
+    public synchronized boolean contains(String target){return unreadable||targets.containsKey(target);}
+    public synchronized boolean absenceObserved(String target){return !unreadable&&Boolean.TRUE.equals(targets.get(target));}
+    public synchronized void require(String target)throws IOException{
+        if(target==null||target.isBlank())return;targets.put(target,false);save();
+    }
+    public synchronized void observe(String transport,Set<String> present)throws IOException{
+        boolean changed=false;
+        for(String key:targets.keySet())if(key.startsWith(transport+":")&&!present.contains(key.substring(transport.length()+1))&&!targets.get(key)){targets.put(key,true);changed=true;}
+        if(changed)save();
+    }
+    public synchronized void confirmed(String target)throws IOException{
+        if(!absenceObserved(target))throw new IOException("尚未观察到该键盘真实离线");
+        Boolean previous=targets.remove(target);try{save();}catch(IOException failure){targets.put(target,previous);throw failure;}
+    }
+    private void save()throws IOException{
+        if(unreadable)throw new IOException("设备恢复记录不可读，写入保持禁用");
+        Files.createDirectories(file.toAbsolutePath().getParent());
+        Path next=Files.createTempFile(file.toAbsolutePath().getParent(),"recovery-",".tmp");
+        try{Files.write(next,JSON.writeValueAsBytes(targets));Files.move(next,file,StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE,StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);}
+        finally{Files.deleteIfExists(next);}
+    }
+}

### src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/service/connection/ManagedBridgeClient.java
--- start/src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/service/connection/ManagedBridgeClient.java
+++ current/src/main/java/com/example/ahakey/service/connection/ManagedBridgeClient.java

+package com.example.ahakey.service.connection;
+
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.node.ObjectNode;
+import java.io.*;
+import java.net.*;
+import java.nio.file.*;
+import java.security.MessageDigest;
+import java.util.*;
+import java.util.concurrent.*;
+import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;
+import java.util.function.Consumer;
+
+/** Versioned, private loopback IPC. No device selection/reconnection is implicit in startup. */
+public final class ManagedBridgeClient implements AutoCloseable {
+    private static final ObjectMapper JSON = new ObjectMapper();
+    private static final class Connection {
+        final Socket socket;
+        Process owner;
+        final ConcurrentMap<String, CompletableFuture<JsonNode>> pending = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
+        final ExecutorService writes = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(r -> daemon(r, "bridge-writes"));
+        volatile String instance = "";
+        long eventSequence;
+        Connection(Socket socket) { this.socket = socket; }
+        void close() {
+            try { socket.close(); } catch (IOException ignored) { }
+            pending.values().forEach(f -> f.completeExceptionally(new IOException("桥连接中断")));
+            writes.shutdownNow();
+        }
+    }
+    private static Thread daemon(Runnable r, String name) { Thread t = new Thread(r, name); t.setDaemon(true); return t; }
+    private final AtomicLong sequence = new AtomicLong();
+    private final ExecutorService events = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(r -> daemon(r, "bridge-events"));
+    private volatile Connection connection;
+    private volatile Process ownedProcess;
+    private volatile boolean closed;
+    private volatile Consumer<JsonNode> eventHandler = event -> {};
+    public void onEvent(Consumer<JsonNode> handler) { eventHandler = Objects.requireNonNull(handler); }
+    public boolean running() { Connection c = connection; return !closed && c != null && !c.socket.isClosed() && !c.instance.isEmpty(); }
+    public static Path applicationDirectory() throws Exception {
+        Path path = Path.of(ManagedBridgeClient.class.getProtectionDomain().getCodeSource().getLocation().toURI());
+        return Files.isRegularFile(path) ? path.getParent() : Path.of(System.getProperty("user.dir"));
+    }
+    public synchronized void start() throws Exception {
+        if (closed) throw new IOException("桥已关闭");
+        if (running()) return;
+        stopOwnedProcess(ownedProcess);
+        if (ownedProcess != null && ownedProcess.isAlive()) throw new IOException("旧桥进程尚未退出");
+        Path root = applicationDirectory();
+        Path executable = root.resolve("BLE_tcp_driver.exe");
+        Path manifest = root.resolve("bridge-managed.json");
+        if (!Files.isRegularFile(executable) || !Files.isRegularFile(manifest)) throw new IOException("随包蓝牙桥版本不匹配，请使用配套安装包。");
+        JsonNode identity = JSON.readTree(Files.readAllBytes(manifest));
+        String hash = HexFormat.of().formatHex(MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(Files.readAllBytes(executable)));
+        if (identity.path("protocolVersion").asInt() != 1 || !hash.equals(identity.path("sha256").asText()))
+            throw new IOException("蓝牙桥身份校验失败，未启动旧桥。");
+        int port;
+        try (ServerSocket reservation = new ServerSocket(0, 1, InetAddress.getLoopbackAddress())) { port = reservation.getLocalPort(); }
+        String token = UUID.randomUUID() + "-" + UUID.randomUUID();
+        ProcessBuilder builder = new ProcessBuilder(executable.toString(), "--studio", "--minimized", "--port", Integer.toString(port));
+        builder.directory(root.toFile());
+        builder.environment().put("AHAKEY_BRIDGE_TOKEN", token);
+        builder.redirectOutput(ProcessBuilder.Redirect.DISCARD); builder.redirectError(ProcessBuilder.Redirect.DISCARD);
+        Process launched = builder.start();
+        ownedProcess = launched;
+        Socket opened = null;
+        try {
+            long deadline = System.nanoTime() + TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(5);
+            while (!closed && opened == null && System.nanoTime() < deadline && launched.isAlive()) {
+                Socket attempt = new Socket();
+                try { attempt.connect(new InetSocketAddress("127.0.0.1", port), 200); opened = attempt; }
+                catch (IOException failure) { attempt.close(); Thread.sleep(50); }
+            }
+            if (closed || opened == null) throw new IOException("蓝牙桥启动超时或端口被占用。");
+            attach(opened);
+            ObjectNode hello = fields(); hello.put("ownerToken", token); hello.putArray("versions").add(1);
+            JsonNode reply = request("hello", hello, 2000);
+            if (reply.path("version").asInt() != 1 || reply.path("bridgeInstanceId").asText().isBlank()
+                    || !reply.path("capabilities").toString().contains("session-writes"))
+                throw new IOException("桥不支持当前连接协议。");
+            connection.instance = reply.path("bridgeInstanceId").asText();
+        } catch (Exception failure) {
+            if (opened != null) try { opened.close(); } catch (IOException ignored) { }
+            stopOwnedProcess(launched);
+            throw failure;
+        }
+    }
+    // Package seam for loopback tests; production calls only after verifying the bundled executable.
+    void attach(Socket opened) throws IOException {
+        if (closed) { opened.close(); throw new IOException("桥已关闭"); }
+        Connection old = connection;
+        if (old != null) old.close();
+        opened.setTcpNoDelay(true);
+        Connection next = new Connection(opened); next.owner=ownedProcess; connection = next;
+        daemon(() -> read(next), "managed-bridge-reader").start();
+    }
+    public JsonNode request(String op, ObjectNode fields, long timeoutMillis) throws Exception {
+        return request(connection, op, fields, timeoutMillis);
+    }
+    public Object ownerIdentity() { return connection; }
+    public boolean ownerRetired(Object identity) {
+        Connection captured=(Connection)identity;
+        return captured==null || (captured.socket.isClosed() && (captured.owner==null || !captured.owner.isAlive()));
+    }
+    /** A failed release retires only the captured owned bridge, never a replacement. */
+    public void releaseSession(Object identity, ObjectNode fields) throws Exception {
+        Connection captured=(Connection)identity;
+        if(captured==null)return;
+        try { request(captured,"disconnect",fields,2000); }
+        catch(Exception failure) {
+            captured.close();stopOwnedProcess(captured.owner);
+            if(captured.owner!=null && captured.owner.isAlive())throw new IOException("旧桥会话尚未确认撤销",failure);
+        }
+    }
+    private JsonNode request(Connection captured, String op, ObjectNode fields, long timeoutMillis) throws Exception {
+        long deadline = System.nanoTime() + TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS.toNanos(timeoutMillis);
+        if (closed || captured == null || captured.socket.isClosed()) throw new IOException("蓝牙桥未连接");
+        String id = Long.toString(sequence.incrementAndGet());
+        ObjectNode request = fields.deepCopy();
+        request.put("version", 1).put("requestId", id).put("bridgeInstanceId", captured.instance).put("op", op);
+        byte[] body = JSON.writeValueAsBytes(request);
+        if (body.length > 16384) throw new IOException("桥请求过长");
+        CompletableFuture<JsonNode> result = new CompletableFuture<>(); captured.pending.put(id, result);
+        try {
+            captured.writes.execute(() -> {
+                try {
+                    if (captured != connection || captured.socket.isClosed() || System.nanoTime() >= deadline)
+                        throw new IOException("桥连接已变化或发送超时");
+                    OutputStream out = captured.socket.getOutputStream();
+                    out.write(0x10); out.write(body.length & 255); out.write(body.length >>> 8); out.write(body); out.flush();
+                } catch (Exception failure) { result.completeExceptionally(failure); }
+            });
+            long remaining = deadline - System.nanoTime();
+            if (remaining <= 0) throw new TimeoutException();
+            JsonNode reply = result.get(remaining, TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS);
+            if (!reply.path("ok").asBoolean()) throw new IOException("蓝牙桥：" + reply.path("code").asText("请求失败"));
+            return reply;
+        } catch (TimeoutException | InterruptedException failure) {
+            // Covers queueing and blocked writes as well as the response. Closing unblocks the writer.
+            captured.close();
+            if (failure instanceof InterruptedException) Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
+            throw new IOException("蓝牙桥请求已中止，连接已撤销：" + op, failure);
+        } finally { captured.pending.remove(id); }
+    }
+    public JsonNode request(String op, long timeoutMillis) throws Exception { return request(op, fields(), timeoutMillis); }
+    public static ObjectNode fields() { return JSON.createObjectNode(); }
+    private void read(Connection captured) {
+        try {
+            DataInputStream in = new DataInputStream(captured.socket.getInputStream());
+            while (!captured.socket.isClosed()) {
+                int type = in.readUnsignedByte(), length = in.readUnsignedByte() | (in.readUnsignedByte() << 8);
+                if (length < 2 || length > 16384 || (type != 0x90 && type != 0x91)) throw new IOException("无效桥封包");
+                byte[] bytes = new byte[length]; in.readFully(bytes);
+                JsonNode value = JSON.readTree(bytes);
+                if (value.path("version").asInt() != 1) throw new IOException("桥版本变化");
+                if (!captured.instance.isEmpty() && !captured.instance.equals(value.path("bridgeInstanceId").asText())) throw new IOException("桥实例变化");
+                if (type == 0x90) {
+                    var future = captured.pending.get(value.path("requestId").asText());
+                    if (future != null) future.complete(value);
+                } else {
+                    long next = value.path("eventSeq").asLong();
+                    if (next <= captured.eventSequence) continue;
+                    captured.eventSequence = next;
+                    dispatch(captured, value);
+                }
+            }
+        } catch (Exception failure) {
+            captured.close();
+            dispatch(captured, fields().put("event", "bridgeDisconnected").put("bridgeInstanceId", captured.instance));
+        }
+    }
+    private void dispatch(Connection captured, JsonNode event) {
+        try { events.execute(() -> { if (connection == captured && !closed) eventHandler.accept(event); }); }
+        catch (RejectedExecutionException ignored) { }
+    }
+    static void stopOwnedProcess(Process process) {
+        if (process == null || !process.isAlive()) return;
+        try {
+            if (process.waitFor(200, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)) return;
+            process.destroy();
+            if (!process.waitFor(200, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)) {
+                process.destroyForcibly(); process.waitFor(600, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
+            }
+        } catch (InterruptedException e) { process.destroyForcibly(); Thread.currentThread().interrupt(); }
+    }
+    public synchronized void restart() throws Exception {
+        Connection previous=connection;connection=null;
+        if(previous!=null)previous.close();stopOwnedProcess(ownedProcess);start();
+    }
+    public synchronized void stop() {
+        Connection previous=connection;connection=null;
+        if(previous!=null)previous.close();stopOwnedProcess(ownedProcess);
+    }
+    public void close() {
+        closed = true;
+        Connection captured = connection;
+        if (captured != null) captured.close();
+        events.shutdownNow();
+        stopOwnedProcess(ownedProcess);
+    }
+}

+    private void refreshRuntimeSlotRows(
+        VBox slotsBox,
+        javafx.scene.control.TreeView<Object> tree,
+        boolean codexHookLatest,
+        boolean cursorHookLatest
+    ) {
+        var service = controller.getAiRuntimeDisplayService();
+        slotsBox.getChildren().clear();
+        ToggleGroup primaryGroup = new ToggleGroup();
+        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
+            final int slotIndex = i;
+            AiRuntimeSlotBinding slot = service.getSlots().get(i);
+            HBox row = new HBox(8);
+            row.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
+
+            RadioButton primary = new RadioButton("主显示");
+            primary.setToggleGroup(primaryGroup);
+            primary.setMinWidth(96);
+            primary.setPrefWidth(96);
+            String unavailableReason = service.slotUnavailableReason(slot, codexHookLatest, cursorHookLatest);
+            boolean primarySelectable = slot.isBound() && unavailableReason == null;
+            primary.setSelected(service.getPrimarySlotIndex() == slotIndex && primarySelectable);
+            primary.setDisable(!primarySelectable);
+            primary.setOnAction(e -> {
+                if (!primary.isDisabled()) {
+                    service.setPrimarySlotIndex(slotIndex);
+                    refreshRuntimeSlotRows(slotsBox, tree, codexHookLatest, cursorHookLatest);
+                }
+            });
+
+            Label summary = new Label(slotSummary(slot) + "｜" + slotAvailabilityText(slot, unavailableReason));
+            summary.getStyleClass().add("group-note");
+            summary.setWrapText(false);
+            summary.setTextOverrun(OverrunStyle.ELLIPSIS);
+            summary.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
+            Tooltip summaryTip = new Tooltip(slotSummary(slot) + "｜" + slotAvailabilityText(slot, unavailableReason));
+            summaryTip.setWrapText(true);
+            summaryTip.setMaxWidth(520);
+            summary.setTooltip(summaryTip);
+
+            Button bind = new Button("绑定到槽位 " + (i + 1));
+            bind.setMinWidth(126);
+            bind.setPrefWidth(126);
+            bind.setOnAction(e -> {
+                Object selected = tree.getSelectionModel().getSelectedItem() == null
+                    ? null
+                    : tree.getSelectionModel().getSelectedItem().getValue();
+                if (selected instanceof AiRuntimeSession session) {
+                    service.bindSlot(slotIndex, session);
+                    tree.refresh();
+                    refreshRuntimeSlotRows(slotsBox, tree, codexHookLatest, cursorHookLatest);
+                    rebuild();
+                }
+            });
+
+            Button clear = new Button("解绑");
+            clear.setMinWidth(72);
+            clear.setPrefWidth(72);
+            clear.setOnAction(e -> {
+                service.clearSlot(slotIndex);
+                tree.refresh();
+                refreshRuntimeSlotRows(slotsBox, tree, codexHookLatest, cursorHookLatest);
+                rebuild();
+            });
+
+            HBox buttons = new HBox(8, bind, clear);
+            buttons.setMinWidth(206);
+            buttons.setPrefWidth(206);
+            row.getChildren().addAll(primary, summary, buttons);
+            HBox.setHgrow(summary, Priority.ALWAYS);
+            slotsBox.getChildren().add(row);
+        }
+    }

+package com.example.ahakey.displayconfig;
