+/** Offline entry point. Never starts the application, HID enumeration, sockets or device commands. */
+public final class DisplayConfigChecks {
+    private static int checks;
+    private static final String HEX = "D7D10510482228B74A2978BD8272F6A4E32668CF6B4F31F491A8503B0DC11E2E";
+    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
+        ((ch.qos.logback.classic.Logger)org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getLogger(org.slf4j.Logger.ROOT_LOGGER_NAME))
+                .setLevel(ch.qos.logback.classic.Level.WARN);
+        ((ch.qos.logback.classic.Logger)org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getLogger(BleManager.class)).setLevel(ch.qos.logback.classic.Level.WARN);
+        protocol(); image(); gif(); state(); lightState(); persistence(); framing(); transport(); coreRegression(); simulation();
+        System.out.println("PASS " + checks + " explicit checks; no hardware/UI/UAT claims");
+    }
+    private static void check(String id, boolean condition) {
+        if (!condition) throw new AssertionError(id);
+        checks++; System.out.println("PASS " + id);
+    }
+    private static void protocol() {
+        for (int i = 0; i < 19; i++) check("A01-capacity-" + i, AhaKeyProtocol.resourceCapacity(i) == (i < 16 && i % 4 == 0 ? 8 : 12));
+        check("A05-begin-independent-bytes", Arrays.equals(AhaKeyProtocol.resourceBegin(18, 12, 0xDDCCBBAAL),
+                new byte[]{(byte)0xAA,(byte)0xBB,(byte)0x8A,18,0,0x40,5,0,12,0,(byte)0xAA,(byte)0xBB,(byte)0xCC,(byte)0xDD,(byte)0xCC,(byte)0xDD}));
+        check("A05-chunk-max", AhaKeyProtocol.resourceChunk(0, 0, new byte[48]).length == 59);
+        rejects("A05-OFF-preview-rejected", () -> AhaKeyProtocol.previewLight(0, 35));
+        rejects("A05-brightness-zero", () -> AhaKeyProtocol.saveDisplayBrightness(0));
+        rejects("A01-invalid-entry", () -> AhaKeyProtocol.resourceCapacity(19));
+        check("A07-OFF-save-valid", AhaKeyProtocol.saveDisplayLight(3, 8, 0)[6] == 0);
+        byte[] integrity = integrity(18, 12, 0xDDCCBBAAL);
+        var parsed = AhaKeyResponseParser.parseDisplayRead(integrity, 3, 18);
+        check("A04-integrity-u32", parsed != null && parsed.crc() == 0xDDCCBBAAL && parsed.bytes() == 344064);
+        check("A04-wrong-target", AhaKeyResponseParser.parseDisplayRead(integrity, 3, 17) == null);
+        check("A04-status-only-fail", AhaKeyResponseParser.parseDisplayRead(ack(0x88, 1), 3, 18) == null);
+        check("A05-status-range", !AhaKeyResponseParser.isDisplayAck(ack(0x8E, 2), (byte)0x8E));
+        CRC32 crc = new CRC32(); crc.update("123456789".getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.US_ASCII));
+        check("A05-known-CRC32", crc.getValue() == 0xCBF43926L);
+    }
+    private static void image() throws Exception {
+        BufferedImage image = new BufferedImage(2, 2, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_ARGB);
+        for (int y = 0; y < 2; y++) for (int x = 0; x < 2; x++) image.setRGB(x, y, 0xffff0000);
+        var encoded = OLEDFrameEncoder.encodeFrame(image);
+        check("A02-size", encoded.rgb565.length == 25600 && encoded.preview.getWidth() == 160 && encoded.preview.getHeight() == 80);
+        check("A02-black-bars", encoded.rgb565[0] == 0 && encoded.preview.getRGB(159, 40) == 0xff000000);
+        int pixel = (40 * 160 + 80) * 2;
+        check("A02-independent-red565", encoded.rgb565[pixel] == (byte)0xF8 && encoded.rgb565[pixel + 1] == 0);
+        check("A02-preview-565", encoded.preview.getRGB(80, 40) == 0xffff0000);
+        byte[] padded = OLEDFrameEncoder.paddedStream(List.of(encoded));
+        check("A05-padding", padded.length == 28672 && Arrays.equals(Arrays.copyOfRange(padded, 25600, padded.length), new byte[3072]));
+        BufferedImage transparent = new BufferedImage(160, 80, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_ARGB);
+        check("A02-transparency-black", OLEDFrameEncoder.encodeFrame(transparent).preview.getRGB(80, 40) == 0xff000000);
+        Path dir = Files.createTempDirectory("wd005-images-");
+        try {
+            Path png = dir.resolve("static.png"); ImageIO.write(image, "png", png.toFile());
+            check("A02-static-one-frame", OLEDFrameEncoder.importFile(png, 8).size() == 1);
+            Path bad = dir.resolve("bad.png"); Files.writeString(bad, "not an image");
+            rejects("A02-corrupt-image", () -> OLEDFrameEncoder.importFile(bad, 8));
+            Path large = dir.resolve("large.png"); Files.write(large, new byte[2 * 1024 * 1024 + 1]);
+            rejects("A02-source-cap", () -> OLEDFrameEncoder.importFile(large, 8));
+        } finally { removeTestDirectory(dir); }
+    }
+    private static OledResourceState.Asset asset() { return OledResourceState.Asset.fromBytes(new byte[28672]); }
+    private static void gif() throws Exception {
+        Path root = Files.createTempDirectory("wd005-gif-");
+        try {
+            for (int count : new int[]{8, 9, 12, 13}) {
+                Path path = root.resolve(count + ".gif");
+                int[][] frames = new int[count][5];
+                for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) frames[i] = new int[]{0, 0, 160, 80, i % 3 + 1};
+                writeGif(path, frames, new String[count]);
+                if (count <= 8) check("A02-GIF-8-full-count", OLEDFrameEncoder.importFile(path, 8).size() == count);
+                else if (count == 9) rejectsMessage("A02-GIF-9-default-rejected", "当前 GIF 有 9 帧，目标位置最多支持 8 帧", () -> OLEDFrameEncoder.importFile(path, 8));
+                if (count <= 12) check("A02-GIF-state-count-" + count, OLEDFrameEncoder.importFile(path, 12).size() == count);
+                else rejectsMessage("A02-GIF-13-rejected", "当前 GIF 有 13 帧", () -> OLEDFrameEncoder.importFile(path, 12));
+            }
+            Path path = root.resolve("disposal.gif");
+            writeGif(path, new int[][]{{0,0,160,80,1}, {40,20,40,40,2}, {80,20,40,40,3}, {0,0,10,10,2}},
+                    new String[]{"doNotDispose", "restoreToPrevious", "restoreToBackgroundColor", "doNotDispose"});
+            var frames = OLEDFrameEncoder.importFile(path, 8);
+            check("A02-GIF-offset-overlay", frames.get(1).preview.getRGB(50,30) == 0xff00ff00 && frames.get(1).preview.getRGB(30,30) == 0xffff0000);
+            check("A02-GIF-disposal3-restore", frames.get(2).preview.getRGB(50,30) == 0xffff0000 && frames.get(2).preview.getRGB(90,30) == 0xff0000ff);
+            check("A02-GIF-disposal2-transparent-black", frames.get(3).preview.getRGB(90,30) == 0xff000000 && frames.get(3).preview.getRGB(50,30) == 0xffff0000);
+            Path basic = root.resolve("no-control.gif");
+            Files.write(basic, Base64.getDecoder().decode("R0lGODdhAQABAIAAAAAAAP///ywAAAAAAQABAAACAkQBADs="));
+            check("A02-GIF-optional-control", OLEDFrameEncoder.importFile(basic, 8).size() == 1);
+        } finally { removeTestDirectory(root); }
+    }
+    private static void writeGif(Path path, int[][] frames, String[] disposals) throws Exception {
+        var writer = ImageIO.getImageWritersByFormatName("gif").next();
+        byte[] red = {0, (byte)255, 0, 0}, green = {0, 0, (byte)255, 0}, blue = {0, 0, 0, (byte)255};
+        var palette = new java.awt.image.IndexColorModel(2, 4, red, green, blue, 0);
+        try (var output = ImageIO.createImageOutputStream(path.toFile())) {
+            writer.setOutput(output);
+            var stream = writer.getDefaultStreamMetadata(writer.getDefaultWriteParam());
+            var tree = (javax.imageio.metadata.IIOMetadataNode)stream.getAsTree("javax_imageio_gif_stream_1.0");
+            var screen = (javax.imageio.metadata.IIOMetadataNode)tree.getElementsByTagName("LogicalScreenDescriptor").item(0);
+            screen.setAttribute("logicalScreenWidth", "160"); screen.setAttribute("logicalScreenHeight", "80");
+            var table = new javax.imageio.metadata.IIOMetadataNode("GlobalColorTable");
+            table.setAttribute("sizeOfGlobalColorTable", "4"); table.setAttribute("backgroundColorIndex", "0"); table.setAttribute("sortFlag", "FALSE");
+            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
+                var color = new javax.imageio.metadata.IIOMetadataNode("ColorTableEntry");
+                color.setAttribute("index", Integer.toString(i)); color.setAttribute("red", Integer.toString(red[i] & 255));
+                color.setAttribute("green", Integer.toString(green[i] & 255)); color.setAttribute("blue", Integer.toString(blue[i] & 255));
+                table.appendChild(color);
+            }
+            tree.appendChild(table);
+            stream.setFromTree("javax_imageio_gif_stream_1.0", tree);
+            writer.prepareWriteSequence(stream);
+            for (int i = 0; i < frames.length; i++) {
+                int[] f = frames[i];
+                var image = new BufferedImage(f[2], f[3], BufferedImage.TYPE_BYTE_INDEXED, palette);
+                for (int y = 0; y < f[3]; y++) for (int x = 0; x < f[2]; x++) image.getRaster().setSample(x, y, 0, f[4]);
+                var metadata = writer.getDefaultImageMetadata(javax.imageio.ImageTypeSpecifier.createFromRenderedImage(image), writer.getDefaultWriteParam());
+                var node = (javax.imageio.metadata.IIOMetadataNode)metadata.getAsTree("javax_imageio_gif_image_1.0");
+                var localTables = node.getElementsByTagName("LocalColorTable");
+                if (localTables.getLength() > 0) node.removeChild(localTables.item(0));
+                var descriptor = (javax.imageio.metadata.IIOMetadataNode)node.getElementsByTagName("ImageDescriptor").item(0);
+                descriptor.setAttribute("imageLeftPosition", Integer.toString(f[0])); descriptor.setAttribute("imageTopPosition", Integer.toString(f[1]));
+                var control = (javax.imageio.metadata.IIOMetadataNode)node.getElementsByTagName("GraphicControlExtension").item(0);
+                control.setAttribute("disposalMethod", disposals[i] == null ? "doNotDispose" : disposals[i]);
+                control.setAttribute("transparentColorFlag", "TRUE"); control.setAttribute("transparentColorIndex", "0");
+                metadata.setFromTree("javax_imageio_gif_image_1.0", node);
+                writer.writeToSequence(new javax.imageio.IIOImage(image, null, metadata), writer.getDefaultWriteParam());
+            }
+            writer.endWriteSequence();
+        } finally { writer.dispose(); }
+    }
+    private static void lightState() {
+        var state = new com.example.ahakey.model.DisplayConfigState();
+        check("A07-unknown-not-OFF", state.effect(0,0) == -1);
+        state.editBrightness(67); state.readBrightness(35);
+        state.editEffect(2,4,0); state.readEffect(2,4,6);
+        check("A07-read-protects-draft", state.brightness.get() == 67 && state.effect(2,4) == 0);
+        state.previewAccepted(4, 67);
+        state.previewUntilNanos.set(System.nanoTime() + 2_000_000_000L);
+        long deadline = state.previewUntilNanos.get();
+        state.message.set("FAIL");
+        check("A08-failure-not-restart", state.devicePreview.get() && state.previewUntilNanos.get() == deadline);
+        state.savedEffect(2,4,0);
+        check("A07-save-responsibility", state.brightnessDirty() && !state.effectDirty(2,4));
+        check("A08-save-ends-preview", !state.devicePreview.get() && state.previewUntilNanos.get() == 0);
+        state.savedBrightness(67);
+        check("A07-brightness-clean", !state.brightnessDirty());
+        Set<Integer> entries = new HashSet<>();
+        for (int mode = 0; mode < 4; mode++) {
+            state.select(mode,0,0); entries.add(state.selectedEntry.get());
+            for (int ai = 0; ai < 3; ai++) { state.select(mode,1,ai); entries.add(state.selectedEntry.get()); state.select(mode,2,ai); entries.add(state.selectedEntry.get()); }
+        }
+        check("A01-selection-19-unique", entries.size() == 19);
+        int frozen = state.selectedEntry.get(); state.busy.set(true); state.select(0,0,0);
+        check("A05-busy-freezes-target", state.selectedEntry.get() == frozen);
+    }
+    private static void state() {
+        var draft = OledResourceState.empty(0).replace(asset());
+        var metadata = new OledResourceState.Metadata(true, 2, 1, 28672, draft.asset().crc());
+        check("A04-refresh-retains-intent", draft.merge(metadata).localStatus() == OledResourceState.LocalStatus.DRAFT);
+        check("A03-clear-device-fact", draft.applied().clear().statusText().equals("设备已有"));
+        check("A04-offline-not-empty", !draft.applied().unknown().deviceKnown() && draft.applied().unknown().asset() != null);
+        check("A04-mismatch-downgrade", draft.applied().merge(new OledResourceState.Metadata(false, 3, 0, 0, 0)).localStatus() == OledResourceState.LocalStatus.DRAFT);
+        check("A06-commit-reconcile", draft.failed("unknown", true).merge(metadata).localStatus() == OledResourceState.LocalStatus.APPLIED);
+        check("A06-begin-fail-not-applied", draft.failed("FAIL", false).merge(metadata).localStatus() == OledResourceState.LocalStatus.FAILED);
+        check("A01-global-name", OledResourceState.empty(16).targetName().equals("全局 MULTI · 运行中"));
+        check("A05-only-draft-upload", draft.canUpload() && !draft.applied().canUpload() && !draft.uploading().canUpload());
+    }
+    private static void persistence() throws Exception {
+        Path root = Files.createTempDirectory("wd005-store-");
+        try {
+            OledResourceStore store = new OledResourceStore(root); store.load();
+            var draft = OledResourceState.empty(4).replace(asset()); store.save(draft);
+            var restored = new OledResourceStore(root).load().get(4);
+            check("A03-restart-no-source", Arrays.equals(restored.asset().bytes(), draft.asset().bytes()) && restored.canUpload());
+            store.save(draft.committing());
+            restored = new OledResourceStore(root).load().get(4);
+            check("A06-crash-before-commit-ack", restored.commitUncertain() && restored.localStatus() == OledResourceState.LocalStatus.FAILED);
+            store.save(draft.uploading());
+            check("A06-crash-before-commit-send", !new OledResourceStore(root).load().get(4).commitUncertain());
+            store.save(draft.clear());
+            check("A03-clear-tombstone", store.containsEntry(4) && new OledResourceStore(root).load().get(4).asset() == null);
+            Files.writeString(root.resolve("index.json"), "broken index");
+            OledResourceStore broken = new OledResourceStore(root);
+            rejects("A03-corrupt-index-rejected", broken::load);
+            rejects("A03-corrupt-index-not-overwritten", () -> broken.save(draft));
+            check("A03-preserve-corrupt-evidence", Files.readString(root.resolve("index.json")).equals("broken index"));
+        } finally { removeTestDirectory(root); }
+    }
+    private static void framing() throws Exception {
+        Method bleFrames = BleManager.class.getDeclaredMethod("reassembleNotifyFrames", byte[].class); bleFrames.setAccessible(true);
+        Method usbFrame = Class.forName("com.example.ahakey.service.UsbHidTransport").getDeclaredMethod("extractFrame", byte[].class, int.class); usbFrame.setAccessible(true);
+        byte[] frame = integrity(18, 12, 0xDDCCBBAAL);
+        for (int split = 1; split < frame.length; split++) {
+            BleManager ble = new OfflineBleManager(callback());
+            List<?> first = (List<?>) bleFrames.invoke(ble, (Object) Arrays.copyOf(frame, split));
+            List<?> second = (List<?>) bleFrames.invoke(ble, (Object) Arrays.copyOfRange(frame, split, frame.length));
+            check("A05-BLE-split-" + split, first.isEmpty() && second.size() == 1 && Arrays.equals((byte[])second.get(0), frame));
+        }
+        byte[] report = new byte[65]; System.arraycopy(frame, 0, report, 1, frame.length);
+        check("A05-USB-binary-delimiters", Arrays.equals((byte[]) usbFrame.invoke(null, report, report.length), frame));
+        byte[] joined = new byte[frame.length + 6]; System.arraycopy(frame, 0, joined, 0, frame.length); System.arraycopy(ack(0x8E, 0), 0, joined, frame.length, 6);
+        check("A05-BLE-coalesced", ((List<?>) bleFrames.invoke(new OfflineBleManager(callback()), (Object) joined)).size() == 2);
+    }
+    private static void transport() throws Exception {
+        FakeSession session = new FakeSession();
+        List<Integer> opcodes = new ArrayList<>();
+        session.respond = request -> {
+            opcodes.add(request[2] & 255);
+            if (request[2] == (byte)0x8B) {
+                try { session.ble.sendCommand(AhaKeyProtocol.setWorkMode(2)); throw new AssertionError("competing write admitted"); }
+                catch (IOException expected) { }
+            }
+            session.reply(ack(request[2] & 255, 0));
+        };
+        List<Integer> progress = new ArrayList<>();
+        OledUploadService.uploadResource(session.ble, 0, 0, asset(), progress::add, () -> {});
+        check("A05-real-upload-chain", opcodes.get(0) == 0x8A && opcodes.get(opcodes.size() - 1) == 0x8C && opcodes.size() == 600);
+        check("A05-progress-acknowledged", progress.get(progress.size() - 1) == 100);
+        for(int frameCount:new int[]{8,12}) {
+            byte[] bytes=new byte[28672*frameCount];
+            for(int f=0;f<frameCount;f++) for(int i=0;i<25600;i++) bytes[f*28672+i]=(byte)(i*31);
+            var content=OledResourceState.Asset.fromBytes(bytes);
+            FakeSession full=new FakeSession(); int[] cursor={0}, commits={0};
+            int entry=frameCount==8?0:18;
+            full.respond=request->{
+                int opcode=request[2]&255;
+                if(opcode==0x8A) {
+                    var header=java.nio.ByteBuffer.wrap(request).order(java.nio.ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN);
+                    if(header.getInt(4)!=bytes.length || Short.toUnsignedInt(header.getShort(8))!=frameCount
+                            || Integer.toUnsignedLong(header.getInt(10))!=content.crc()) throw new AssertionError("Begin header");
+                } else if(opcode==0x8B) {
+                    int offset=java.nio.ByteBuffer.wrap(request).order(java.nio.ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN).getInt(4),length=request[8]&255;
+                    if(offset!=cursor[0] || length<1 || length>48 || request[3]!=entry
+                            || !Arrays.equals(Arrays.copyOfRange(request,9,9+length),Arrays.copyOfRange(bytes,offset,offset+length))) throw new AssertionError("Chunk continuity/payload");
+                    cursor[0]+=length;
+                } else if(opcode==0x8C) { if(cursor[0]!=bytes.length) throw new AssertionError("early Commit"); commits[0]++; }
+                else throw new AssertionError("unexpected command");
+                full.reply(ack(opcode,0));
+            };
+            OledUploadService.uploadResource(full.ble,0,entry,content,p->{},()->{});
+            check("A05-full-"+frameCount+"-frames-offset-payload-CRC",cursor[0]==bytes.length && commits[0]==1);
+        }
+        FakeSession disk = new FakeSession();
+        int[] idle = {0}; disk.ble.setDisplayIdleListener(() -> idle[0]++);
+        disk.respond = request -> disk.reply(ack(request[2] & 255, 0));
+        rejects("A06-local-persist-failure", () -> OledUploadService.uploadResource(disk.ble, 0, 0, asset(), p -> {}, () -> { throw new IOException("disk full"); }));
+        check("A06-quarantine-before-idle-flush", idle[0] == 0 && disk.ble.isDisplayWriteUncertain());
+        FakeSession changed = new FakeSession();
+        Method reset = BleManager.class.getDeclaredMethod("resetDisplaySession"); reset.setAccessible(true);
+        reset.invoke(changed.ble);
+        rejects("A06-reconnect-drops-admission", () -> changed.ble.openDisplayLease(true));
+        rejects("A06-frozen-generation", () -> changed.ble.openDisplayLease(false, 0));
+        Method notify = BleManager.class.getDeclaredMethod("onSessionNotify", long.class, byte[].class); notify.setAccessible(true);
+        notify.invoke(changed.ble, 0L, ack(0x8E,0));
+        check("A06-old-generation-not-delivered", changed.ble.connectionGeneration() == 1);
+        FakeSession fail = new FakeSession(); fail.respond = request -> fail.reply(ack(request[2] & 255, 1));
+        rejects("A06-Begin-FAIL", () -> OledUploadService.uploadResource(fail.ble, 0, 0, asset(), p -> {}, () -> {}));
+        FakeSession timeout = new FakeSession(); timeout.respond = request -> {};
+        try (var lease = timeout.ble.openDisplayLease(true)) {
+            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
+            rejects("A06-interrupted-request", () -> timeout.ble.exchangeDisplay(lease, AhaKeyProtocol.previewBrightness(35)));
+            Thread.interrupted();
+        }
+        // Real timeout: intentionally allow the actual 15-second wait, not a copied/mock timeout implementation.
+        try (var lease = timeout.ble.openDisplayLease(true)) {
+            rejects("A06-ACK-timeout", () -> timeout.ble.exchangeDisplay(lease, AhaKeyProtocol.saveDisplayBrightness(35)));
+        }
+        timeout.reply(ack(0x8E, 0));
+        rejects("A06-late-ACK-quarantine", () -> timeout.ble.openDisplayLease(true));
+        check("A06-write-unknown", timeout.ble.isDisplayWriteUncertain());
+    }
+    private static void coreRegression() throws Exception {
+        check("A10-status-query-wire", Arrays.equals(AhaKeyProtocol.queryDeviceStatus(),hex("aabb00ccdd")));
+        check("A10-mode-wire", Arrays.equals(AhaKeyProtocol.setWorkMode(3),hex("aabb9203ccdd")));
+        check("A10-key-read-wire", Arrays.equals(AhaKeyProtocol.readOrdinaryKeyConfig(2,3),hex("aabb870203ccdd")));
+        check("A10-side-read-wire", Arrays.equals(AhaKeyProtocol.readSideSwitchConfig(),hex("aabb97ccdd")));
+        check("A10-sleep-read-wire", Arrays.equals(AhaKeyProtocol.querySleepWaitTime(),hex("aabb95ccdd")));
+        check("A10-identity-read-wire", Arrays.equals(AhaKeyProtocol.queryFirmwareCatalogKey(),hex("aabb9fccdd")));
+        check("A10-runtime-wire", Arrays.equals(AhaKeyProtocol.updateState((byte)4),hex("aabb9004ccdd")));
+        Method reassemble=BleManager.class.getDeclaredMethod("reassembleNotifyFrames",byte[].class); reassemble.setAccessible(true);
+        byte[] legacy=hex("aabb9200ccdd");
+        List<?> frames=(List<?>)reassemble.invoke(new OfflineBleManager(callback()),(Object)legacy);
+        check("A10-legacy-ACK-framing",frames.size()==1 && Arrays.equals((byte[])frames.get(0),legacy));
+        check("A10-legacy-ACK-parser",AhaKeyResponseParser.parseCommandResponse(legacy).status()==0);
+        var state=new com.example.ahakey.model.StudioState();
+        state.updateKeyDescription(com.example.ahakey.model.StudioPart.KEY2,"retained-key");
+        String before=new com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper().writeValueAsString(state.toPersisted());
+        state.displayConfig().editBrightness(81); state.displayConfig().editEffect(0,0,4);
+        state.displayConfig().put(OledResourceState.empty(0).replace(asset()));
+        check("A10-other-drafts-not-mutated",before.equals(new com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper().writeValueAsString(state.toPersisted())));
+        var restored=new com.example.ahakey.model.StudioState();
+        restored.loadFromPersisted(new com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper().readValue(before,com.example.ahakey.model.StudioState.PersistedDraft.class));
+        check("A10-key-draft-roundtrip",restored.getKeyConfig(com.example.ahakey.model.StudioPart.KEY2).getDescription().equals("retained-key"));
+        FakeSession session=new FakeSession(); List<byte[]> requests=new ArrayList<>(); session.respond=request->{requests.add(request);session.reply(ack(request[2]&255,0));};
+        try(var lease=session.ble.openDisplayLease(true)) {
+            session.ble.sendCommand(AhaKeyProtocol.queryDeviceStatus());
+            for(int opcode:new int[]{0x84,0x85,0x87,0x90,0x91,0x92,0x95,0x96,0x97,0x98,0x99,0x9f})
+                rejects("A10-lease-rejects-"+Integer.toHexString(opcode),()->session.ble.sendCommand(AhaKeyProtocol.buildFrame((byte)opcode,new byte[0])));
+            rejects("A10-lease-rejects-raw-data",()->session.ble.writeData(new byte[]{1}));
+            check("A10-only-status-passes",requests.size()==1 && requests.get(0)[2]==0);
+            check("A10-runtime-first-deferred",!session.ble.sendRuntimeState((byte)1));
+            check("A10-runtime-latest-deferred",!session.ble.sendRuntimeState((byte)4));
+            Field pending=BleManager.class.getDeclaredField("deferredRuntimeState"); pending.setAccessible(true);
+            check("A10-runtime-latest-only",(Byte)pending.get(session.ble)==4);
+        }
+        check("A10-runtime-flushed-once",requests.size()==2 && Arrays.equals(requests.get(1),hex("aabb9004ccdd")));
+    }
+    private static byte[] hex(String value) { return java.util.HexFormat.of().parseHex(value); }
+    private static void simulation() {
+        // Literal goldens calculated from pinned psk_ws2812.c initial phases and update_bit integer scaling.
+        // They are not generated by FirmwareLightSimulator or reused production helpers.
+        int[][] firstTick100 = {
+            {0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000},
+            {0x03071f,0x01020a,0x000105,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000},
+            {0x1b0000,0x090700,0x020500,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000},
+            {0x150000,0x151100,0x0b1500,0x001505,0x001516,0x000615,0x0a0015,0x150011},
+            {0x150000,0x151100,0x0b1500,0x001505,0x001516,0x000615,0x0a0015,0x150011},
+            {0x060c32,0x060c32,0x060c32,0x060c32,0x060c32,0x060c32,0x060c32,0x060c32},
+            {0x000000,0x000002,0x01020b,0x030619,0x030619,0x01020b,0x000002,0x000000},
+            {0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x031f25,0x031f25,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000},
+            {0x1f0903,0x110502,0x080201,0x030100,0x010000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000},
+            {0x0d0d10,0x00090b,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000},
+            {0x000000,0x000000,0x000002,0x000206,0x000206,0x000002,0x000000,0x000000},
+            {0x1d0a00,0x1d0300,0x1d0a00,0x1d0300,0x1d0a00,0x1d0300,0x1d0a00,0x1d0300},
+            {0x081408,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000},
+            {0x000003,0x000106,0x000208,0x00020a,0x00030d,0x00030f,0x00030e,0x00030c},
+            {0x1d0000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x1d0000},
+            {0x081408,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000,0x000000},
+            {0x050200,0x050200,0x050200,0x141108,0x141108,0x050200,0x050200,0x050200},
+        };
+        for (int effect=0;effect<firstTick100.length;effect++)
+            check("A08-golden-initial-100-"+effect, Arrays.equals(new FirmwareLightSimulator().step(effect,100),firstTick100[effect]));
+        var move = new FirmwareLightSimulator();
+        for(int tick=0;tick<7;tick++) move.step(1,100);
+        check("A08-golden-right-boundary",Arrays.equals(move.step(1,100),new int[]{0,0,0,0,0,0x000105,0x01020a,0x03071f}));
+        check("A08-golden-bounce",Arrays.equals(move.step(1,100),new int[]{0,0,0,0,0x000105,0x01020a,0x03071f,0x01020a}));
+        check("A08-golden-brightness-35",Arrays.equals(new FirmwareLightSimulator().step(1,35),new int[]{0x01020a,0x000003,0x000002,0,0,0,0,0}));
+        // Independent expected output from firmware integer equations at the initial tick, brightness=100.
+        check("A08-OFF", Arrays.equals(new FirmwareLightSimulator().step(0, 100), new int[8]));
+        check("A08-single-move-initial", Arrays.equals(new FirmwareLightSimulator().step(1, 100), new int[]{0x03071f,0x01020a,0x000105,0,0,0,0,0}));
+        check("A08-breath-alpha-zero-firmware-semantics", new FirmwareLightSimulator().step(5, 100)[0] == 0x060c32);
+        check("A08-warning-initial", new FirmwareLightSimulator().step(11, 100)[0] == 0x1d0a00);
+        for (int effect = 0; effect <= 16; effect++) {
+            var simulator = new FirmwareLightSimulator();
+            for (int tick = 0; tick < 300; tick++) {
+                int[] pixels = simulator.step(effect, 35);
+                if (pixels.length != 8 || Arrays.stream(pixels).anyMatch(p -> p < 0 || p > 0xffffff)) throw new AssertionError("A08-range");
+            }
+            check("A08-eight-lights-range-" + effect, true);
+        }
+    }
+    private static byte[] ack(int opcode, int status) { return AhaKeyProtocol.buildFrame((byte)opcode, new byte[]{(byte)status}); }
+    private static byte[] integrity(int entry, int frames, long crc) {
+        var data = java.nio.ByteBuffer.allocate(13).order(java.nio.ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN);
+        data.put((byte)0).put((byte)3).put((byte)entry).putShort((short)frames).putInt(frames * 28672).putInt((int)crc);
+        return AhaKeyProtocol.buildFrame((byte)0x88, data.array());
+    }
+    private interface Checked { void run() throws Exception; }
+    private static void rejectsMessage(String id, String text, Checked action) {
+        try { action.run(); } catch (Exception expected) { check(id, expected.getMessage().contains(text)); return; }
+        throw new AssertionError(id + " did not reject");
+    }
+    private static void rejects(String id, Checked action) {
+        try { action.run(); } catch (Exception expected) { check(id, true); return; }
+        throw new AssertionError(id + " did not reject");
+    }
+    private static BleManager.BleCallback callback() {
+        return new BleManager.BleCallback() {
+            public void onConnected() {} public void onDisconnected() {}
+            public void onStatusReceived(com.example.ahakey.model.DeviceStatus status) {}
+            public void onError(String message) {}
+        };
+    }
+    static final class FakeSession {
+        final OfflineBleManager ble = new OfflineBleManager(callback());
+        Consumer<byte[]> respond;
+        final Method notify;
+        FakeSession() throws Exception {
+            this(true);
+        }
+        FakeSession(boolean admitted) throws Exception {
+            ble.usb.commands = request -> respond.accept(request);
+            notify = BleManager.class.getDeclaredMethod("processNotifyFrame", byte[].class); notify.setAccessible(true);
+            field("isConnected", true); ble.getCachedStatus().setConnected(true);
+            field("outputStream", new ByteArrayOutputStream() {
+                @Override public synchronized void write(byte[] bytes, int offset, int length) {
+                    byte[] request = Arrays.copyOfRange(bytes, offset + 3, offset + length);
+                    respond.accept(request);
+                }
+            });
+            if (admitted) identify();
+        }
+        void identify() throws Exception {
+            Consumer<byte[]> previous = respond;
+            respond = request -> reply(AhaKeyProtocol.buildFrame((byte)0x9F,
+                    ("\0x1-c582-hw1-p1-1.0.0-r001").getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.US_ASCII)));
+            try { ble.readFirmwareCatalogKey(); } finally { respond = previous; }
+        }
+        void useUsb() { ble.usb.open(this::reply); }
+        void field(String name, Object value) throws Exception { Field field = BleManager.class.getDeclaredField(name); field.setAccessible(true); field.set(ble, value); }
+        void reply(byte[] bytes) { try { notify.invoke(ble, (Object)bytes); } catch (Exception e) { throw new IllegalStateException(e); } }
+    }
+    private static void removeTestDirectory(Path directory) throws IOException {
+        try (var files = Files.walk(directory)) {
+            for (Path path : files.sorted(Comparator.reverseOrder()).toList()) Files.delete(path);
+        }
+    }
+}

+package com.example.ahakey.displayconfig;
+
+import com.example.ahakey.service.BleManager;
+import com.example.ahakey.service.UsbHidTransport;
+import java.io.IOException;
+import java.lang.reflect.Field;
+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.Collections;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;
+import java.util.function.Consumer;
+
+/** Every fixture uses this constructor before any production send or lease operation. */
+final class OfflineBleManager extends BleManager {
+    final MemoryUsb usb = new MemoryUsb();
+    final AtomicInteger discoveries = new AtomicInteger();
+    final AtomicInteger blockedConnections = new AtomicInteger();
+    volatile boolean usbPresent;
+
+    OfflineBleManager(BleCallback callback) throws ReflectiveOperationException {
+        super(callback);
+        Field transport = BleManager.class.getDeclaredField("usbTransport");
+        transport.setAccessible(true);
+        transport.set(this, usb);
+    }
+
+    @Override protected void transmitFirmware(UsbHidTransport transport,String session,String target,long generation,byte[] frame,boolean data) throws Exception {
+        if(transport.isOpen()){if(data)transport.sendData(frame);else transport.sendCommand(frame);return;}
+        Field field=BleManager.class.getDeclaredField("outputStream");field.setAccessible(true);
+        var output=(java.io.OutputStream)field.get(this);
+        if(output==null)throw new IOException("Offline transport disconnected");
+        output.write(com.example.ahakey.protocol.BleTcpPacket.encode(data?com.example.ahakey.protocol.BleTcpPacket.WRITE_DATA:com.example.ahakey.protocol.BleTcpPacket.WRITE_COMMAND,frame));
+    }
+    @Override protected boolean isUsbDevicePresent() {
+        discoveries.incrementAndGet();
+        return usbPresent;
+    }
+
+    @Override public void connect() {
+        blockedConnections.incrementAndGet();
+        throw new AssertionError("Real connection forbidden in offline fixture");
+    }
+
+    static final class MemoryUsb extends UsbHidTransport {
+        private volatile boolean opened;
+        volatile Consumer<byte[]> commands = frame -> { throw new AssertionError("Unexpected USB command"); };
+        final AtomicInteger opens = new AtomicInteger();
+        final AtomicInteger closes = new AtomicInteger();
+        final List<byte[]> sent = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>());
+        final List<byte[]> data = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>());
+
+        @Override public void open(Consumer<byte[]> onFrame) {
+            opens.incrementAndGet();
+            opened = true;
+        }
+        @Override public boolean isOpen() { return opened; }
+        @Override public void close() {
+            closes.incrementAndGet();
+            opened = false;
+        }
+        @Override public void sendCommand(byte[] frame) throws IOException {
+            if (!opened) throw new IOException("Memory USB closed");
+            sent.add(frame.clone());
+            commands.accept(frame.clone());
+        }
+        @Override public void sendData(byte[] bytes) throws IOException {
+            if (!opened) throw new IOException("Memory USB closed");
+            data.add(bytes.clone());
+        }
+    }
+}

+public final class Wd007DraftReadbackChecks {
+    static void connect(Fixture f,String transport,String id,int key)throws Exception{
+        idle(f.controller);

+            Thread.sleep(10);
+        }
+        check("actual connection performs all ordinary readbacks "+transport+id,f.wire.frames.stream().filter(b->b[2]==(byte)0x87).count()>=16);
+        idle(f.controller);fx(()->null);
+    }

+        public final K1Route[] routes=new K1Route[4];
+        public final String[][] descriptions=new String[4][4];
+        public final byte[][][] actions=new byte[4][4][];
+        public final int[][] types=new int[4][4];
+        public final List<byte[]> frames=new java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
+        public boolean legacy,missingBank,badReadCrc,uncertainCommit,failBegin;public int commits,begins;
+        public Runnable onCommit=()->{},onLegacyRead=()->{};
+        private byte[] pending;private int token,mode,offset;private long crc;
+        public Wire(BleManager.BleCallback callback)throws Exception {
+            super(callback);
+            for(int m=0;m<4;m++){routes[m]=consumer(0xE9+m);for(int k=0;k<4;k++){actions[m][k]=new byte[]{(byte)(4+m+k)};types[m][k]=0x73;descriptions[m][k]="device-"+m+"-"+k;}}
+            usb.consumer=this::respond;

+        public static K1Route consumer(int usage){byte[] b=new byte[102];b[0]=1;b[1]=2;b[2]=(byte)usage;b[3]=(byte)(usage>>8);return new K1Route(b);}
+        @Override public void queryStatus(){}
+        private void respond(byte[] f){try {
+            frames.add(f.clone());int opcode=f[2]&255;
+            if(opcode!=0x9e){byte[] payload={0};
+                if(opcode==0x87){int m=f[3]&255,k=f[4]&255;byte[] desc=descriptions[m][k].getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.US_ASCII),action=actions[m][k];if(action.length+desc.length>53){reply(AhaKeyProtocol.buildFrame((byte)0x87,new byte[]{3}));return;}payload=new byte[6+action.length+desc.length];payload[1]=(byte)m;payload[2]=(byte)k;payload[3]=(byte)types[m][k];payload[4]=(byte)action.length;System.arraycopy(action,0,payload,5,action.length);payload[5+action.length]=(byte)desc.length;System.arraycopy(desc,0,payload,6+action.length,desc.length);if(k==0&&legacy)onLegacyRead.run();}
+                else if(opcode==0x73){int m=f[4]&255,k=f[5]&255;byte[] body=Arrays.copyOfRange(f,6,f.length-2);if(f[3]==0x75)descriptions[m][k]=new String(body,java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.US_ASCII);else{types[m][k]=f[3]&255;actions[m][k]=body;}}
+                else if(opcode==0x97)payload=new byte[]{0,0,2,0,0,1,0};
+                else if(opcode==0x9f)payload=com.example.ahakey.firmware.FirmwareCatalog.CATALOG.getBytes();
+                reply(AhaKeyProtocol.buildFrame(f[2],payload));return;
+            }
+            int sub=f[4]&255;var b=ByteBuffer.wrap(f,5,f.length-7).slice().order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN);
+            if(legacy){reply(AhaKeyProtocol.buildFrame((byte)0x9e,new byte[]{0}));return;}
+            int correlation=sub==0x20?b.getInt(7):b.getInt(0);
+            if((missingBank&&sub==0x10)||(failBegin&&sub==0x20)){reply(response(sub,1,K1RouteProtocol.fields(4).putInt(correlation).array()));return;}
+            byte[] fields;
+            switch(sub){

+                case 0x10:{int m=b.get(4)&255,o=b.get(5)&255,n=b.get(6)&255;byte[] route=routes[m].bytes();fields=K1RouteProtocol.fields(12+n).putInt(correlation).put((byte)m).put((byte)o).putShort((short)102).putInt((int)K1RouteProtocol.crc(route)+(badReadCrc?1:0)).put(route,o,n).array();break;}
+                case 0x20:{begins++;mode=b.get()&255;int total=b.getShort()&65535;crc=Integer.toUnsignedLong(b.getInt());token=b.getInt();check("begin is single mode, exact 102 bytes, nonzero token",mode<4&&total==102&&token!=0);pending=new byte[total];offset=0;fields=K1RouteProtocol.fields(10).putInt(token).putInt(token).putShort((short)0).array();break;}

+        }catch(Exception e){throw new RuntimeException(e);}}
+    }

+            var lock=(java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock)lockField.get(ble);Object monitor=monitorField.get(ble);var idle=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean();
+            ble.setDisplayIdleListener(()->{check("idle callback outside command and transaction locks",!lock.isHeldByCurrentThread()&&!Thread.holdsLock(monitor));idle.set(true);});
+            var transaction=java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture.runAsync(()->{try{ble.k1RouteTransaction(0,()->{byte[] result=ble.exchangeK1Route(K1RouteProtocol.request(0,K1RouteProtocol.fields(4).putInt(77).array()),0);K1RouteProtocol.capability(result,77);return null;});if(outcome.equals("failure")||outcome.equals("generation"))throw new AssertionError("expected transaction failure");}catch(Exception e){if(!outcome.equals("failure")&&!outcome.equals("generation"))throw new RuntimeException(e);}});
+            check("actual K1 wait entered "+outcome,waiting.await(2,java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS));
+            rejects("maintenance cannot enter active K1 transaction",()->ble.maintenance().begin(DeviceMaintenanceChecks.identity(),DeviceMaintenanceChecks.HASH,"fixture",false));
+            for(var state:List.of(com.example.ahakey.model.IDEState.USER_PROMPT_SUBMIT,com.example.ahakey.model.IDEState.TASK_COMPLETED))check("SINGLE Hook pending during K1 "+outcome+state,runtime.handleHookEventResult("Kimi",state,null)==com.example.ahakey.service.AiRuntimeDisplayService.Delivery.PENDING);
+            check("no runtime writes while K1 owns transaction",sent.stream().noneMatch(f->f[2]==(byte)0x90));
+            if(outcome.equals("expired"))DeviceMaintenanceChecks.field(ble,"deferredRuntimeAt",System.nanoTime()-java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.MINUTES.toNanos(6));
+            if(outcome.equals("generation")){var reset=BleManager.class.getDeclaredMethod("resetDisplaySession");reset.setAccessible(true);reset.invoke(ble);}
+            ble.reply(outcome.equals("failure")?response(0,1,K1RouteProtocol.fields(4).putInt(77).array()):response(0,0,K1RouteProtocol.fields(6).putInt(77).put((byte)3).put((byte)4).array()));
+            transaction.get(4,java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS);
+            check("success and failed K1 transactions notify safe release "+outcome,idle.get());
+            var writes=sent.stream().filter(f->f[2]==(byte)0x90).toList();
+            check("release flushes latest once unless expired or cross-generation "+outcome,outcome.equals("expired")||outcome.equals("generation")?writes.isEmpty():writes.size()==1&&writes.get(0)[3]==com.example.ahakey.model.IDEState.TASK_COMPLETED.getCode());
+            var op=ble.maintenance().begin(DeviceMaintenanceChecks.identity(),DeviceMaintenanceChecks.HASH,"fixture",false);int before=sent.size();
+            DeviceMaintenanceChecks.field(ble,"deferredRuntimeState",(byte)6);DeviceMaintenanceChecks.field(ble,"deferredRuntimeAt",System.nanoTime());var flush=BleManager.class.getDeclaredMethod("flushDeferredRuntimeState");flush.setAccessible(true);flush.invoke(ble);
+            ble.maintenance().finish(op,true);flush.invoke(ble);check("maintenance invalidates pending fact without later replay",sent.size()==before);
+        }
+    }

+    static final class Memory extends UsbHidTransport {
+        boolean open;Consumer<byte[]> receive;String id;final Wire owner;
+        Memory(Wire owner){this.owner=owner;}
+        @Override public void openExact(String path,Consumer<byte[]> consumer){id=path;receive=consumer;open=true;owner.opened.put(path,this);}
+        @Override public boolean isOpen(){return open;}
+        @Override public String devicePath(){return id;}
+        @Override public void close(){open=false;}
+        @Override public void cancelPendingIo(){super.cancelPendingIo();}
+        void failReader(){open=false;readerTerminatedUnexpectedly();}
+        @Override public void sendCommand(byte[] frame){
+            owner.frames.add(id+":"+(frame[2]&255));
+            if((frame[2]&255)==owner.held){owner.heldRequest=frame.clone();owner.heldMemory=this;return;}
+            answer(frame);
+        }
+        void answer(byte[] f){
+            byte[] payload=f[2]==0?new byte[]{80,1,1,2,0,0,(byte)owner.position,35}:f[2]==(byte)0x9F?"\0x1-c582-hw1-p1-1.0.0-r001".getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.US_ASCII):f[2]==(byte)0x88?new byte[]{0,2,f[4],0,0}:new byte[]{0};
+            if(f[2]==(byte)0x97&&f.length==5)payload=new byte[]{0,0,2,0,0,2,0};
+            if(f[2]==(byte)0x87)payload=new byte[]{0,f[3],f[4],0x73,1,(byte)owner.readbackHid,0};
+            receive.accept(AhaKeyProtocol.buildFrame(f[2],payload));
+        }

+package com.example.ahakey.service;

+import com.example.ahakey.model.connection.DeviceTarget;
+import com.example.ahakey.service.connection.ManagedBridgeClient;

+/** Simulated process liveness only; actual private client/release barrier and USB selection. */
+public final class Wd007BridgeRetirementChecks {
+    static void owner(ManagedBridgeClient client,Process process)throws Exception{var f=ManagedBridgeClient.class.getDeclaredField("ownedProcess");f.setAccessible(true);f.set(client,process);}
+    static void attach(ManagedBridgeClient client,Socket socket)throws Exception{var m=ManagedBridgeClient.class.getDeclaredMethod("attach",Socket.class);m.setAccessible(true);m.invoke(client,socket);}
+    public static void main(String[] args)throws Exception{
+        System.setProperty("user.home",Files.createTempDirectory("wd007-release-retry-").toString());Wire w=new Wire();StubbornProcess old=new StubbornProcess(),replacement=new StubbornProcess();
+        try(var listener=new ServerSocket(0,2,InetAddress.getLoopbackAddress());var oldSocket=new Socket("127.0.0.1",listener.getLocalPort());var oldPeer=listener.accept();var newSocket=new Socket("127.0.0.1",listener.getLocalPort());var newPeer=listener.accept()){
+            ManagedBridgeClient client=(ManagedBridgeClient)field(w,"managedBridge");owner(client,old);attach(client,oldSocket);Object oldIdentity=client.ownerIdentity();oldSocket.close();
+            Wd007SafetyChecks.set(w,"activeTarget",new DeviceTarget("BLE","old-A","A"));Wd007SafetyChecks.set(w,"bridgeSession","old-session");w.disconnect();
+            await(()->{try{return ((java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture<?>)field(w,"bridgeRelease")).isCompletedExceptionally();}catch(Exception e){throw new RuntimeException(e);}},"owned process remains alive after bounded release");
+            w.candidates=List.of("B");w.connectSelected(new DeviceTarget("USB","B","B"));await(()->!w.isScanning(),"first explicit retry finishes");
+            check(!w.isConnected()&&!client.ownerRetired(oldIdentity),"live old owner still blocks new connection");
+            owner(client,replacement);attach(client,newSocket);Object newIdentity=client.ownerIdentity();
+            old.alive=false;
+            w.connectSelected(new DeviceTarget("USB","B","B"));await(w::isConnected,"later explicit connect retires historical failed barrier after actual exit");
+            check(w.targetIdentity().equals("USB:B")&&client.ownerRetired(oldIdentity),"B connected without restarting Studio");
+            check(!newSocket.isClosed()&&!client.ownerRetired(newIdentity)&&replacement.destroys==0,"retiring old barrier leaves replacement process and connection untouched");
+            check(!((java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture<?>)field(w,"bridgeRelease")).isCompletedExceptionally(),"failed release barrier replaced after positive retirement proof");
+            System.out.println("PASS "+checks+" late process exit/replacement checks; Process substitutes and loopback only");
+        }finally{old.alive=false;replacement.alive=false;w.shutdownTransport();}
+    }

+package com.example.ahakey.service;
+
+import com.example.ahakey.model.connection.DeviceTarget;
+import com.example.ahakey.protocol.AhaKeyProtocol;
+import java.io.*;
+import java.nio.file.*;
+import java.util.*;
+import java.util.concurrent.*;
+import static com.example.ahakey.service.Wd007BehaviorChecks.*;
+
+/** Real admission, owned-release, observation and generated wrapper paths; memory devices only. */
+public final class Wd007SafetyChecks {
+    static void set(Object object,String name,Object value)throws Exception{var f=BleManager.class.getDeclaredField(name);f.setAccessible(true);f.set(object,value);}
+    static Wire connected()throws Exception{
+        System.setProperty("user.home",Files.createTempDirectory("wd007-safety-").toString());
+        Wire w=new Wire();w.connectSelected(new DeviceTarget("USB","A","A"));await(w::isConnected,"fixture connected");return w;
+    }
+    static void queued(boolean timeout)throws Exception{
+        Wire w=connected();var pool=Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2);try{
+            w.held=0x73;
+            var first=pool.submit(()->{try{w.sendCommand(AhaKeyProtocol.buildFrame((byte)0x73,new byte[]{0}));return false;}catch(IOException expected){return true;}});
+            await(()->w.heldMemory!=null,"first physical request held");
+            var thread=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<Thread>();
+            var second=pool.submit(()->{thread.set(Thread.currentThread());try{w.sendCommand(AhaKeyProtocol.queryDeviceStatus());return false;}catch(IOException expected){return true;}});
+            await(()->thread.get()!=null&&thread.get().getState()==Thread.State.TIMED_WAITING,"second request entered ACK wait queue");
+            if(!timeout)w.cancelDeviceOperation();
+            check(first.get(18,TimeUnit.SECONDS)&&second.get(2,TimeUnit.SECONDS),"both pending operations fail after "+(timeout?"timeout":"cancel"));
+            w.heldMemory.answer(w.heldRequest);
+            check(w.frames.equals(List.of("A:115")),"queued request never reaches physical sender, even with late ACK");
+            check(!w.refreshAutoApproval(),"quarantine cannot auto approve");
+        }finally{w.shutdownTransport();pool.shutdownNow();}
+    }
+    static void releaseAndObservation()throws Exception{
+        Wire w=connected();try{
+            String attachment=w.draftIdentity();w.observeUsbCandidates(Set.of("A"));
+            // Emulate a failed release being safely retired by the actual managed client (null means never had a child).
+            set(w,"bridgeSession","dead-session");set(w,"activeTarget",new DeviceTarget("BLE","old","A"));
+            w.disconnect();w.candidates=List.of("A","B");w.connectSelected(new DeviceTarget("USB","B","B"));
+            await(()->w.isConnected()&&w.targetIdentity().equals("USB:B"),"dead owned bridge does not permanently block USB B");
+            w.observeUsbCandidates(Set.of("A","B"));

+                }

+            }
+        }finally{server.stop();w.shutdownTransport();}
+    }

+package com.example.ahakey.service;

+public final class Wd007UsbTerminationChecks {
+    static final class ReaderProbe extends UsbHidTransport {void failed(){readerTerminatedUnexpectedly();}}
+    static Runnable callback(UsbHidTransport transport)throws Exception{
+        var field=UsbHidTransport.class.getDeclaredField("disconnectHandler");field.setAccessible(true);return (Runnable)field.get(transport);
+    }
+    static void connected(StudioController controller,Wire w)throws Exception{
+        w.frames.clear();w.connectSelected(new DeviceTarget("USB","A","same path, no serial"));
+        await(w::isConnected,"manual configuration connect");
+        readback(controller,w);
+    }
+    static void readback(StudioController controller,Wire w)throws Exception{
+        long end=System.nanoTime()+TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(5);
+        while(System.nanoTime()<end){
+            if(w.frames.stream().filter(s->s.equals("A:135")).count()>=16&&fx(()->!controller.getStudioState().inputReadbackInProgressProperty().get()&&!controller.getStudioState().k1Routes().busy.get()))return;
+            Thread.sleep(10);
+        }
+        throw new AssertionError("controller did not complete readback");
+    }
+    static void atomicTermination()throws Exception{
+        var workers=Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2);
+        try{for(int i=0;i<40;i++){
+            ReaderProbe probe=new ReaderProbe();var calls=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger();probe.onUnexpectedDisconnect(calls::incrementAndGet);var start=new CountDownLatch(1);
+            var close=workers.submit(()->{start.await();probe.cancelPendingIo();return null;});
+            var fail=workers.submit(()->{start.await();probe.failed();return null;});start.countDown();close.get(2,TimeUnit.SECONDS);fail.get(2,TimeUnit.SECONDS);
+            check(calls.get()==(probe.terminatedUnexpectedly()?1:0),"atomic cause and notification agree under simultaneous close/failure "+i);
+            boolean cause=probe.terminatedUnexpectedly();probe.cancelPendingIo();probe.failed();
+            check(probe.terminatedUnexpectedly()==cause,"terminal cause cannot be overwritten "+i);
+        }}finally{workers.shutdownNow();}
+    }
+    public static void main(String[] args)throws Exception{
+        var calls=new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger();ReaderProbe probe=new ReaderProbe();probe.onUnexpectedDisconnect(calls::incrementAndGet);probe.failed();probe.failed();
+        check(calls.get()==1,"unexpected terminal step reports once");
+        ReaderProbe closing=new ReaderProbe();closing.onUnexpectedDisconnect(calls::incrementAndGet);closing.close();closing.failed();
+        check(calls.get()==1,"intentional cancellation does not report unexpected termination");
+        atomicTermination();
+        System.setProperty("user.home",Files.createTempDirectory("wd007-usb-termination-").toString());Platform.startup(()->{});Platform.setImplicitExit(false);
+        Wire[] wires=new Wire[1];StudioController controller=fx(()->new StudioController(cb->wires[0]=new Wire(cb),ble->new KimiAhaKeyBridge(ble,0),false));
+        var pool=Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor();
+        try{
+            Wire w=wires[0];connected(controller,w);String a=w.draftIdentity();
+            fx(()->{controller.getStudioState().getKeyConfig(ModeSlot.MODE0,StudioPart.KEY2).setHidCode(31);controller.getStudioState().markDirty(StudioPart.KEY2);return null;});
+            w.disconnect();check(!w.isConnected(),"explicit disconnect stays disconnected");connected(controller,w);
+            check(a.equals(w.draftIdentity())&&fx(()->controller.getStudioState().getKeyConfig(ModeSlot.MODE0,StudioPart.KEY2).getHidCode()==31),"manual disconnect/reconnect of still-observed attachment retains A draft");
+            Memory old=w.opened.get("A");Runnable late=callback(old);old.failReader();
+            check(!w.isConnected()&&w.getCachedStatus().getSavedBindings()==null,"reader terminal synchronously revokes current connection and approval facts");
+            fx(()->null);check(!fx(()->controller.getDeviceStatus().isConnected()),"actual controller shows disconnected after reader failure");
+            check(w.candidates.equals(List.of("A")),"enumeration remained identical throughout fast replacement");
+            w.pollUsbCandidates();check(!w.isConnected(),"same-path observation never auto reconnects failed reader");

+            var drafts=StudioState.class.getDeclaredField("deviceDrafts");drafts.setAccessible(true);

+            late.run();check(w.isConnected()&&w.connectionGeneration()==generation&&w.draftIdentity().equals(b),"late old-reader callback cannot disconnect or retire B");
+            fx(()->{controller.getStudioState().getKeyConfig(ModeSlot.MODE0,StudioPart.KEY2).setHidCode(44);controller.getStudioState().markDirty(StudioPart.KEY2);return null;});
+            Memory queuedReader=w.opened.get("A");
+            var commandLock=(java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock)field(w,"commandLock");
+            Thread reader=new Thread(queuedReader::failReader,"fixture-reader-terminal");
+            String replacementIdentity;long replacementGeneration;
+            commandLock.lock();try{
+                reader.start();

+                w.frames.clear();w.readbackHid=8;

+public final class Wd007WindowExitChecks {
+    static class Probe extends App {final CountDownLatch exited=new CountDownLatch(1);@Override protected void exitRuntime(){exited.countDown();}}
+    static <T>T fx(Callable<T> call)throws Exception{var done=new CompletableFuture<T>();Platform.runLater(()->{try{done.complete(call.call());}catch(Throwable e){done.completeExceptionally(e);}});return done.get(8,TimeUnit.SECONDS);}
+    static void set(Object value,String name,Object data)throws Exception{var field=App.class.getDeclaredField(name);field.setAccessible(true);field.set(value,data);}
+    static void invoke(App app,String name)throws Exception{var method=App.class.getDeclaredMethod(name);method.setAccessible(true);method.invoke(app);}
+    public static void main(String[] args)throws Exception{

+            StudioController controller=fx(()->new StudioController(cb->wire,KimiAhaKeyBridge::new,false));

+            await(()->service.state().ready.get(),"local library ready");
+            Probe app=new Probe();Stage stage=fx(()->{
+                service.connectionChanged();var current=service.state().current().replace(OledResourceState.Asset.fromBytes(new byte[28672]));service.state().put(current);
+                Stage window=new Stage();window.setScene(new Scene(new Pane(),320,160));set(app,"controller",controller);set(app,"primaryStage",window);invoke(app,"installWindowCloseHandler");

+            });
+            wire.held=0x8B;fx(()->{service.upload(service.state().current());return null;});await(()->wire.heldRequest!=null,"real display upload awaits chunk ACK");
+            fx(()->{stage.fireEvent(new WindowEvent(stage,WindowEvent.WINDOW_CLOSE_REQUEST));return null;});

+            check(!fx(stage::isShowing)&&controller.getStudioState().uploadingOledProperty().get(),"X hides to tray and preserves pending upload");
+            long at=System.nanoTime();fx(()->{controller.requestApplicationExit();return null;});
+            check(fx(()->true),"FX responsive during exit cleanup");check(app.exited.await(6,TimeUnit.SECONDS),"actual menu exit reaches termination within bounded cleanup");
+            check(TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(System.nanoTime()-at)<6000,"exit deadline measured");

+package com.example.ahakey.service.connection;
+
+import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.*;
+import java.io.*;
+import java.net.*;
+import java.util.concurrent.*;
+import java.util.concurrent.atomic.*;
+
+/** Real loopback framing plus blocked-stream fixtures; never starts the bridge or a device. */
+public final class ManagedBridgeClientChecks {
+    static final ObjectMapper JSON=new ObjectMapper();
+    static int checks;
+    public static final class StubbornProcess extends Process {
+        public volatile boolean alive=true;
+        public int destroys;
+        public OutputStream getOutputStream(){return OutputStream.nullOutputStream();}
+        public InputStream getInputStream(){return InputStream.nullInputStream();}
+        public InputStream getErrorStream(){return InputStream.nullInputStream();}
+        public int waitFor(){if(alive)throw new IllegalStateException("still alive");return 0;}
+        public boolean waitFor(long timeout,TimeUnit unit){return !alive;}
+        public int exitValue(){if(alive)throw new IllegalThreadStateException();return 0;}
+        public void destroy(){destroys++;}
+        public Process destroyForcibly(){destroys++;return this;}
+        public boolean isAlive(){return alive;}
+    }
+    static void check(boolean value,String name){if(!value)throw new AssertionError(name);checks++;}
+    static JsonNode read(Socket s)throws Exception{
+        DataInputStream in=new DataInputStream(s.getInputStream());check(in.readUnsignedByte()==0x10,"request envelope");
+        int n=in.readUnsignedByte()|in.readUnsignedByte()<<8;return JSON.readTree(in.readNBytes(n));
+    }
+    static void send(Socket s,int type,JsonNode node)throws Exception{
+        byte[] b=JSON.writeValueAsBytes(node);var out=s.getOutputStream();out.write(type);out.write(b.length&255);out.write(b.length>>8);out.write(b);out.flush();
+    }
+    static class BlockedSocket extends Socket {
+        final CountDownLatch entered=new CountDownLatch(1),released=new CountDownLatch(1);
+        volatile boolean shut;
+        @Override public void setTcpNoDelay(boolean ignored){}
+        @Override public boolean isClosed(){return shut;}
+        @Override public void close(){shut=true;released.countDown();}
+        void block()throws IOException{try{released.await();}catch(InterruptedException e){Thread.currentThread().interrupt();}throw new IOException("closed");}
+        @Override public InputStream getInputStream(){return new InputStream(){public int read()throws IOException{block();return -1;}};}
+        @Override public OutputStream getOutputStream(){return new OutputStream(){public void write(int b)throws IOException{entered.countDown();block();}};}
+    }
+    public static void main(String[] args)throws Exception{
+        try(var client=new ManagedBridgeClient();var listener=new ServerSocket(0,1,InetAddress.getLoopbackAddress());var local=new Socket("127.0.0.1",listener.getLocalPort());var peer=listener.accept()){
+            client.attach(local);
+            var pool=Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2);
+            try{
+                var first=pool.submit(()->client.request("first",1000));JsonNode a=read(peer);
+                var second=pool.submit(()->client.request("second",1000));JsonNode b=read(peer);
+                send(peer,0x90,ManagedBridgeClient.fields().put("version",1).put("bridgeInstanceId","one").put("requestId",b.path("requestId").asText()).put("ok",true).put("value",2));
+                send(peer,0x90,ManagedBridgeClient.fields().put("version",1).put("bridgeInstanceId","one").put("requestId",a.path("requestId").asText()).put("ok",true).put("value",1));
+                check(first.get().path("value").asInt()==1 && second.get().path("value").asInt()==2,"correlation survives out of order replies");
+            }finally{pool.shutdownNow();}
+        }
+        try(var client=new ManagedBridgeClient()){
+            BlockedSocket socket=new BlockedSocket();client.attach(socket);long at=System.nanoTime();
+            try{client.request("blocked",120);throw new AssertionError("deadline missing");}catch(IOException expected){}
+            check(TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(System.nanoTime()-at)<800,"blocked write has total deadline");
+            check(socket.shut,"deadline closes owning socket");
+        }
+        try(var client=new ManagedBridgeClient();var listener=new ServerSocket(0,2,InetAddress.getLoopbackAddress())){
+            var entered=new CountDownLatch(1);var release=new CountDownLatch(1);var delivered=new AtomicInteger();
+            client.onEvent(event->{if(event.path("event").asText().equals("hold")){entered.countDown();try{release.await();}catch(InterruptedException e){Thread.currentThread().interrupt();}}else delivered.incrementAndGet();});
+            try(var a=new Socket("127.0.0.1",listener.getLocalPort());var pa=listener.accept();var b=new Socket("127.0.0.1",listener.getLocalPort());var pb=listener.accept()){
+                client.attach(a);send(pa,0x91,ManagedBridgeClient.fields().put("version",1).put("eventSeq",1).put("event","hold"));
+                check(entered.await(1,TimeUnit.SECONDS),"event queue deliberately held");
+                pa.close();Thread.sleep(30);client.attach(b);release.countDown();Thread.sleep(70);
+                check(delivered.get()==0,"queued old disconnect cannot reach new connection");
+                send(pb,0x91,ManagedBridgeClient.fields().put("version",1).put("eventSeq",1).put("event","new"));
+                long end=System.nanoTime()+TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(1);while(delivered.get()==0&&System.nanoTime()<end)Thread.sleep(5);
+                check(delivered.get()==1,"new connection owns independent event sequence");
+            }finally{release.countDown();}
+        }
+        try(var client=new ManagedBridgeClient()){
+            BlockedSocket old=new BlockedSocket();client.attach(old);Object identity=client.ownerIdentity();old.close();
+            BlockedSocket replacement=new BlockedSocket();client.attach(replacement);
+            client.releaseSession(identity,ManagedBridgeClient.fields().put("sessionId","A"));
+            check(!replacement.shut,"failed old release cannot close new bridge connection");
+            client.releaseSession(identity,ManagedBridgeClient.fields().put("sessionId","A"));
+            check(!replacement.shut,"retired release is repeatable, no exceptional barrier");
+        }
+        System.out.println("PASS "+checks+" managed IPC behavior checks; loopback only");
+    }
+}

+package com.example.ahakey.service;
+
+import java.io.*;
+import java.net.*;
+import java.lang.reflect.Field;
+import java.util.Arrays;
+
+/** Isolated loopback ports only; never calls connect(), HID discovery, or the application. */
+public final class KimiBridgeLifecycleCheck {
+    private static int checks;
+    private static final class Transport extends BleManager {
+        long generation = 1;
+        boolean connected = true;
+        boolean usb = true;
+        Transport() { super(null);getCachedStatus().setConnected(true);getCachedStatus().setSavedBindings(Wd007BehaviorChecks.side(true));getCachedStatus().setSwitchState(0); }
+        @Override public boolean refreshAutoApproval(){return connected&&getCachedStatus().isAutoApproval();}
+        @Override public long connectionGeneration() { return generation; }
+        @Override public boolean isConnected() { return connected; }
+        @Override public boolean isUsbConnected() { return usb; }
+        @Override public void connect() { throw new AssertionError("Hardware forbidden"); }
+        @Override protected boolean isUsbDevicePresent() { throw new AssertionError("HID forbidden"); }
+    }
+    private static Object field(Object value, String name) throws Exception {
+        Field field = value.getClass().getDeclaredField(name);
+        field.setAccessible(true);
+        return field.get(value);
+    }
+    private static int port(KimiAhaKeyBridge bridge) throws Exception {
+        return ((ServerSocket) field(field(bridge, "session"), "listener")).getLocalPort();
+    }
+    private static void check(boolean condition, String message) {
+        if (!condition) throw new AssertionError(message);
+        checks++;
+    }
+    private static Socket client(int port) throws Exception {
+        Socket socket = new Socket("127.0.0.1", port);
+        socket.setSoTimeout(2000);
+        return socket;
+    }
+    private static byte[] query(Socket client, int type) throws Exception {
+        client.getOutputStream().write(new byte[]{(byte)type, 0, 0});
+        DataInputStream in = new DataInputStream(client.getInputStream());
+        int response = in.readUnsignedByte();
+        int length = in.readUnsignedByte() | in.readUnsignedByte() << 8;
+        check(response == (type == 3 ? 0x82 : 0x83), "unchanged response type");
+        return in.readNBytes(length);
+    }
+    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
+        if(args.length==2 && args[0].equals("consumer-probe")) {
+            Transport fixture=new Transport();
+            switch(args[1]) {
+                case "swapped-auto" -> {fixture.getCachedStatus().setSavedBindings(Wd007BehaviorChecks.side(false));fixture.getCachedStatus().setSwitchState(1);}
+                case "custom" -> fixture.getCachedStatus().setSwitchState(1);
+                case "unknown" -> fixture.getCachedStatus().clearSavedBindings();
+                case "disconnected" -> fixture.connected=false;
+                case "auto" -> {}
+                default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException();
+            }
+            KimiAhaKeyBridge probe=new KimiAhaKeyBridge(fixture,0);try{probe.start();System.out.println("KIMI_PROBE_PORT="+port(probe));System.out.flush();System.in.read();}finally{probe.close();}return;
+        }
+        Transport transport = new Transport();
+        KimiAhaKeyBridge bridge = new KimiAhaKeyBridge(transport, 0);
+        try {
+            bridge.connectionChanged();
+            for (int round = 0; round < 30; round++) {
+                int oldPort = port(bridge);
+                try (Socket oldClient = client(oldPort)) {
+                    check(Arrays.equals(query(oldClient, 3), new byte[]{1,0,0,1}), "status payload");
+                    bridge.prepareForConnection();
+                    oldClient.setSoTimeout(200);
+                    check(oldClient.getInputStream().read() == -1, "stop closes accepted clients immediately");
+                    // Same-generation late connect callback cannot reclaim the BLE port.
+                    bridge.connectionChanged();
+                    check(field(bridge,"session") == null, "late USB callback blocked while switching");
+                    try (ServerSocket bleListener = new ServerSocket()) {
+                        bleListener.setReuseAddress(true);
+                        bleListener.bind(new InetSocketAddress("127.0.0.1", oldPort));
+                        transport.generation++;
+                        transport.usb = false;
+                        bridge.connectionChanged();
+                        check(field(bridge,"session") != null, "BLE now uses Java compatibility listener too");
+                    }
+                    transport.generation++;
+                    transport.usb = true;
+                    bridge.connectionChanged();
+                    Object newSession = field(bridge,"session");
+                    bridge.connectionChanged(); // delayed disconnect notification reconciles current USB
+                    check(field(bridge,"session") == newSession, "late disconnect cannot stop current USB");
+                    try (Socket fresh = client(port(bridge))) {
+                        query(fresh, 3);
+                        byte[] info = query(fresh, 4);
+                        check(info.length == 10 && info[6] == 0, "USB reconnect preserves Kimi auto state");
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+            transport.connected = false;
+            bridge.connectionChanged();
+            check(field(bridge,"session") == null, "disconnect stops service");
+            bridge.stop(); bridge.stop();
+            transport.connected = true;
+            transport.generation++;
+            bridge.connectionChanged();
+            bridge.close();
+            bridge.connectionChanged(); bridge.start();
+            check(field(bridge,"session") == null, "shutdown rejects late start");
+        } finally { bridge.close(); }
+        try (ServerSocket occupied = new ServerSocket(0,10,InetAddress.getLoopbackAddress())) {
+            int busyPort = occupied.getLocalPort();
+            KimiAhaKeyBridge retry = new KimiAhaKeyBridge(transport, busyPort);
+            try {
+                retry.start();
+                check(field(retry,"session") == null, "occupied external port not taken over");
+                occupied.close();
+                retry.start();
+                try (Socket socket = client(busyPort)) { query(socket, 3); query(socket, 4); }
+            } finally { retry.close(); }
+        }
+        System.out.println("PASS " + checks + " Kimi checks; 30 USB/BLE/USB rounds; ephemeral ports only");
+    }
 }
